It uses only public extension UI and\nlifecycle hooks. Core tools are not replaced or wrapped, and the editor stays\nPi's own component: Aurora installs no editor of its own, so editing, history,\ncompletion, shortcuts and the `editorPaddingX` / `autocompleteMaxVisible`\nsettings all come from the runtime (see\n`docs/decisions/013-aurora-keeps-the-native-editor.md`).\n\nThe theme is `themes/aurora-night.json`. Motion and the dashboard presentation\nare read from the effective central setup configuration (`ui.motion`,\n`ui.dashboard`). One shared ticker runs only while work is visible. Only real\nmoving work animates: in `contextual`, active thinking and running tools cycle\ntheir glyph every 100 ms, while `ANTWORTET` and `WARTET AUF MODELL` keep a fixed\nglyph and repaint once per second for the elapsed time and the `WARTET AUF\nMODELL` transition alone.\n\n- `contextual`: animated activity indicator.\n- `reduced`: static activity indicator.\n- `off`: no animated indicator; activity text remains available.\n\n## The permanent surfaces\n\n**Footer** (`footer.ts`) — the one permanent status surface, and one line.\nIt shows the workflow, model, thinking level, session folder, context share and\nverification state, then drops whole segments from the least important end as\nthe terminal narrows. From comfortable width on, the workflow and every risk\nsegment render as filled status chips (pills); routine metadata stays flat so\nthe line never turns into a wall of colour, and narrow tiers keep the flat look\nentirely. Whenever a visible dashboard surface owns verification reporting\n(auto/compact/expanded), the footer suppresses the routine successful state;\nfailed or stale checks remain critical footer risks at every width. The folder\nis derived from the session CWD captured at session start and compacted purely;\nit never probes the filesystem. Risk segments — YOLO, a failed verification, a\nbroken language server — ignore the width tier and are the last thing dropped.\nSize classes come from `shared/layout.ts`, shared with the menu shell.\n\n`renderFooterLines` is pure. Everything it prints was already in runtime state:\nit starts no process, probes neither git nor the LSP, asks no provider and reads\nno file. It is called on every frame, so anything else would be paid for\ncontinuously.\n\n**Session dashboard** (`tool-renderers.ts`) — permanent, above the editor, with\nfour presentation modes owned by the single setting `ui.dashboard` in setup.json\n(`auto|compact|expanded|hidden`, default `auto`), switched through `/dashboard`\nwhich surfaces in the Super+Q command center — no new shortcut:\n\n- **`auto`** is the responsive permanent default (`renderAutoDashboard`): after\n  the fresh-session welcome it keeps a compact session card with task,\n  activity, changes and verification visible, including during idle — a filled\n  tile that takes the error surface when verification fails. Failed or stale\n  verification appears before routine information; narrow terminals fall back\n  to at most two unframed rows while keeping risks visible.\n- **`compact`** caps the dashboard at two hard rows.\n- **`expanded`** keeps the richer multi-tile view (Aufgabe/Aktivität/\n  Änderungen/Prüfungen) within a tested height budget of roughly 40 % of\n  terminal rows. From `wide` width on the tiles form a two-column card grid\n  (Aufgabe + Aktivität, Änderungen + Prüfungen); a pair costs the height of\n  its taller member, so the failure verdict still outranks routine tiles.\n- **`hidden`** emits no dashboard at all while workflow state, footer risks and\n  the inspector stay fully alive.\n\nPhase and verification verdict are derived separately but share one staleness\ndefinition (`verificationIsStale()`): `done` requires idle plus a current\n`READY` check, only a real running verification tool shows `Prüfen`, and active\nwork stays `Arbeiten` even after an earlier failed check — see\n[decision 019](../../docs/decisions/019-dashboard-modes-and-phase-precedence.md).\nThe status labels, tone mapping and tile rendering live in `tool-renderers.ts`\nand `tile.ts`; `index.ts` only derives the existing runtime view model and\napplies the terminal row budget. Finished tools still leave the live activity\nlist immediately; their real change and verification results remain visible\nthrough the relevant session tiles. The welcome is never shown again within\nthat session and is skipped for resumed conversations.\n\n**Visual language** (`tile.ts`) — dashboard, welcome window and inspector\nrender as filled cards: framed tiles whose title row and body rows are padded\nand painted through `Theme.bg`, plus labelled fields and status pills instead\nof loose text. Backgrounds never use hardcoded ANSI colours — Pi's public\n`Theme.bg` accepts exactly eight surfaces (`selectedBg`, `scrollbarThumb`,\n`searchMatchBg`, `userMessageBg`, `customMessageBg`, `toolPendingBg`,\n`toolSuccessBg`, `toolErrorBg`), and the tile primitives map tones onto them\n(neutral card: `toolPendingBg`, success: `toolSuccessBg`, error: `toolErrorBg`,\naccent chips: `selectedBg`, warning: inverse), so every theme including\n`light` stays correct. Under 18 columns tiles fall back to frameless rows;\ncompact surfaces stay flat by design.\n\nDer Header zeigt während eines laufenden Turns `DENKT NACH` (mit Thinking-Level),\n`ARBEITET`, `ANTWORTET` oder nach vier Sekunden ohne konkretes Aurora-Ereignis\n`WARTET AUF MODELL`, jeweils mit einer Laufzeit. `WARTET AUF MODELL` bedeutet,\ndass weder ein Tool noch ein asynchroner Subagent läuft und der Turn nur auf\ndie nächste Rückmeldung vom Modell/Provider wartet: Die Animation bleibt ein\nLebenszeichen, keine Hänger- oder Fehlerdiagnose, macht aber jetzt explizit,\n_worauf_ gewartet wird. `idle` wird nur beim tatsächlichen Turnabschluss\n(`agent_settled`) gesetzt, sofern keine asynchronen Subagenten weiterarbeiten.\n`agent_end` beendet nur einen einzelnen Agentenlauf; Pi kann danach noch\nautomatisch retryen, kompaktieren oder einen weiteren Lauf starten.\n\nEin Async-Subagent, der gestartet, aber noch im Status `queued` ist, zeigt in\nseiner Kachel dagegen `IM HINTERGRUND` statt `WARTET` — bewusst ein anderer\nText als der Turn-Header, da beide unterschiedliche Sachverhalte beschreiben\n(Turn wartet auf das Modell vs. Subagent läuft bereits im Hintergrund) und\nzuvor identische Strings die beiden leicht verwechselbar machten.\n\n`tool_execution_start` ist Auroras einzige Quelle für eine Toolzeile. Es ordnet\ndie realen Laufzeitdaten als Lesen, Suchen, Bearbeiten, Shell, Testen, Prüfen,\nLSP, Subagent oder Werkzeug ein; bei unbekannten Werkzeugen bleibt zusätzlich\nder echte Toolname sichtbar. Kein Tool wird von der UI gewrappt oder aufgerufen. Bekannte LSP-Namen erscheinen nur als LSP-Aktivität, wenn dieses\nTool tatsächlich startet. LSP-Gesundheit bleibt eine Footer-Angelegenheit. Eine\nlaufende Verifikation nutzt den aktiven Kreis statt eines Erfolgs-Häkchens;\nabgeschlossene Tools verschwinden aus der transienten Anzeige und Pis\nErgebnisausgabe bleibt die Quelle der Wahrheit.\n\nForeground subagents come from the `subagent` tool call itself. Async entries\ncome from the subagent package's `subagent:async-started` and\n`subagent:async-complete` lifecycle events; a `subagent:control-event` can mark\na known async agent as needing attention. Aurora does not send the package's\nstatus RPC and therefore cannot initiate a status tool call. The active\nconfiguration keeps the Fleet Status Dock disabled, so Aurora owns the compact\nsubagent view inside its session dashboard.\n\n## UI state event contract\n\nImport the channel and message types from `state.ts`. On every session start,\nAurora emits `aurora-ui/state/request` with a new `sessionEpoch`. Providers\nanswer on `aurora-ui/state/snapshot`, then publish later changes on\n`aurora-ui/state/patch`. Aurora discards snapshots and patches from older\nepochs. `publishAuroraUiPatch` and `publishAuroraUiSnapshot` are the typed\npublisher helpers.\n\nBesides `workflow`, `permissions`, `lsp`, `model` and `activity`, two owner\ngroups feed the task-centric view (`task-projection.ts`) and the inspector:\n\n- `changes` — published by `extensions/diff-viewer/index.ts` after every\n  recorded edit/write, aggregated straight from its `ChangeTracker` (real\n  per-file diff stats, never estimated). Drives the Änderungen tile and the\n  Inspector's Changes section.\n- `verification` — published by `extensions/setup-core/index.ts` alongside\n  its existing `ctx.ui.setStatus(\"verification\", …)` calls. Carries the\n  structured per-profile outcome (`declaredRequiredIds`, `requiredOutcomes`,\n  `blockingRecommendedIds`), not just the coarse status string, so\n  `projectVerificationState` can derive real checks/evidence instead of\n  three hardcoded, always-`passed` criteria.\n\nBoth are `null` until their owning extension has something to report — Aurora\nnever fabricates a value for either.\n\nCleanup on session replacement, reload and shutdown restores the core footer\nand working indicator, removes the widget, unsubscribes from the event bus,\ncancels any pending subagent request and stops the shared ticker.\n\n## Render diagnostics (development only)\n\n`dev-diagnostics.ts` counts widget renders, approximates their duration,\nreports the active tick interval and the last dashboard row count. It is inert\nunless `PI_AURORA_DIAG=1` is set at process start; normal sessions pay nothing.\nThe runtime suite's `[render-measure]` line records the current cost of one full\nwidget frame (~0.9 ms with four active tools) — far below the 100 ms tick\nbudget, which is why frame caching and a slower ticker were deliberately not\nbuilt (decision 019).\n\n## The inspector (third, on-demand surface)\n\n`/inspect` (`inspector-command.ts`, catalogued under the `code` category, so\nit surfaces through the existing Super+Q command center — no new shortcut, no\ncommand palette) opens Pi's native selector with six entries: Changes,\nKontext, Verification Evidence, Modelle, Reasoning, Diagnostics. Picking one\nrenders `renderInspectorBox` (`inspector.ts`) — the one shared box shell for\nsecondary information — and shows it via `ctx.ui.notify`, the same\nintegration style `/setup-doctor context` already uses.\n\nIt reads state, never owns it: Changes and Verification reuse the same\n`state.changes` / task `verification` the main view already shows; Kontext and\nDiagnostics call `collectContextDiagnostics`\n(`extensions/setup-core/context-diagnostics.ts`) directly, on demand only —\nnever from the per-frame footer/widget render path, since it walks the full\nsession history. The Kontext section deliberately shows only what the runtime\nreports exactly (token/window counts, deterministic byte counts, compaction\ntimestamps, cumulative lifetime usage) and never a per-category breakdown\n(conversation/files/tool results/memory): that would need a token estimate\nper category the runtime cannot provide without guessing.\n\n### Aurora's activity widget hands\n * the subagent lines over instead of printing a second, poorer copy.\n *\n * Read from the subagent extension's own config rather than from a shared\n * setting: the dock is opt-in there, and only that file knows whether it is on.\n * Any read or parse problem means \"no dock\", i.e. the widget keeps reporting.\n * This runs once per session start, never from a render.\n */\nfunction fleetDockOwnsSubagentDisplay(): boolean {\n  try {\n    const raw = JSON.parse(readFileSync(SUBAGENT_CONFIG_PATH, \"utf8\")) as {\n      ui?: { fleetView?: unknown };\n    };\n    return raw.ui?.fleetView === true;\n  } catch {\n    return false;\n  }\n}\n\ntype SubagentToolArgs = {\n  action?: unknown;\n  async?: unknown;\n  agent?: unknown;\n  tasks?: unknown;\n  chain?: unknown;\n};\n\nfunction record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {\n  return value && typeof value === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(value)\n    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)\n    : undefined;\n}\n\nfunction agentNamesFromTaskList(value: unknown): string[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];\n  return value.flatMap((item) => {\n    const task = record(item);\n    if (!task) return [];\n    const agent = typeof task.agent === \"string\" ? task.agent : undefined;\n    const parallel = agentNamesFromTaskList(task.parallel);\n    return [...(agent ? [agent] : []), ...parallel];\n  });\n}\n\nfunction foregroundSubagentsFromArgs(\n  args: unknown,\n  runId: string,\n): SubagentInfo[] {\n  const input = args as SubagentToolArgs | undefined;\n  if (!input || input.action !== undefined || input.async === true) return [];\n  const agents = [\n    ...(typeof input.agent === \"string\" ? [input.agent] : []),\n    ...agentNamesFromTaskList(input.tasks),\n    ...agentNamesFromTaskList(input.chain),\n  ];\n  return [...new Set(agents.length > 0 ? agents : [\"subagent\"])].map(\n    (agent) => ({\n      agent,\n      runId,\n      status: \"running\",\n    }),\n  );\n}\n\ntype AsyncSubagentStartEvent = {\n  id?: unknown;\n  sessionId?: unknown;\n  agent?: unknown;\n  agents?: unknown;\n  chain?: unknown;\n  mode?: unknown;\n};\n\ntype SubagentControlEvent = {\n  type?: unknown;\n  agent?: unknown;\n  runId?: unknown;\n};\n\nfunction stringList(value: unknown): string[] {\n  return Array.isArray(value)\n    ? value.filter(\n        (item): item is string => typeof item === \"string\" && Boolean(item),\n      )\n    : [];\n}\n\n/**\n * The subagent package publishes this when a background run is actually\n * launched. Keeping Aurora on that event avoids asking its status RPC, whose\n * handler executes a second, UI-initiated subagent action.\n */\nfunction asyncSubagentsFromStart(\n  value: unknown,\n  sessionId: string | undefined,\n): { runId: string; entries: SubagentInfo[] } | undefined {\n  const event = record(value) as AsyncSubagentStartEvent | undefined;\n  const runId = event?.id;\n  if (!event || typeof runId !== \"string\") return undefined;\n  if (typeof event.sessionId === \"string\" && event.sessionId !== sessionId)\n    return undefined;\n  const agents = [\n    ...stringList(event.agents),\n    ...stringList(event.chain),\n    ...(typeof event.agent === \"string\" ? [event.agent] : []),\n  ];\n  const unique = [...new Set(agents)];\n  return {\n    runId,\n    entries: (unique.length > 0 ? unique : [\"async\"]).map((agent) => ({\n      agent,\n      runId,\n      // The package's own async tracker initially records this event as\n      // queued. Do not invent a stronger lifecycle state here.\n      status: \"queued\",\n    })),\n  };\n}\n\nfunction asyncCompletionId(\n  value: unknown,\n  sessionId: string | undefined,\n): string | undefined {\n  const event = record(value);\n  if (!event || typeof event.id !== \"string\") return undefined;\n  if (typeof event.sessionId === \"string\" && event.sessionId !== sessionId)\n    return undefined;\n  return event.id;\n}\n\nfunction attentionFromControlEvent(\n  value: unknown,\n): { runId: string; agent: string } | undefined {\n  const event = record(record(value)?.event) as\n    SubagentControlEvent | undefined;\n  if (\n    !event ||\n    event.type !== \"needs_attention\" ||\n    typeof event.runId !== \"string\" ||\n    typeof event.agent !== \"string\"\n  )\n    return undefined;\n  return { runId: event.runId, agent: event.agent };\n}\n\nfunction makeEpoch(sequence: number): string {\n  return `${Date.now().toString(36)}-${sequence.toString(36)}`;\n}\n\nfunction makeState(\n  epoch: string,\n  ctx: ExtensionContext,\n  pi: ExtensionAPI,\n): AuroraUiState {\n  return {\n    sessionEpoch: epoch,\n    workflow: { phase: \"work\", label: \"Work\" },\n    permissions: {},\n    lsp: {},\n    model: {\n      id: ctx.model?.id,\n      thinking: String(pi.getThinkingLevel()),\n    },\n    activity: { kind: \"idle\" },\n    changes: null,\n    verification: null,\n    task: { title: \"Aktuelle Aufgabe\", phaseLabel: \"Bereit\" },\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/layout.ts\nimport { truncateToWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\n\n/**\n * Aurora's width-safe truncation, accounting for ANSI escapes and wide glyphs.\n *\n * The size classes this file used to define moved to `shared/layout.ts`, where\n * the menu shell reaches them too. Only the truncation stayed behind: it is the\n * one piece that needs `pi-tui` at module scope, and `shared/layout.ts` has to\n * remain import-free so the menu modules can use it under the jiti test loader.\n *\n * `truncateToWidth` wraps its injected ellipsis in full resets\n * (`\\x1b[0m…\\x1b[0m`) and may preserve the original closing codes after it.\n * That punches an unfilled hole into a tile row and can leave the preceding\n * `\\x1b[38;...m` or `\\x1b[1m` unclosed; the unclosed codes then bleed into the\n * next segment, frame, or neighbouring tile. This wrapper removes the reset\n * artifacts, keeps any suffix that still fits, and re-balances the visible\n * prefix by appending only the closes that are actually missing\n * (`\\x1b[39m` for foreground, `\\x1b[22m` for bold).\n */\n\ninterface StyleBalance {\n  fgOpens: number;\n  fgCloses: number;\n  boldOpens: number;\n  boldCloses: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Counts the foreground and bold codes a string leaves open.\n *\n * Pi's theme picks its escape form from the detected colour depth, so the same\n * `theme.fg(...)` call emits `\\x1b[38;2;r;g;bm` on a truecolor terminal,\n * `\\x1b[38;5;Nm` on a 256-colour one and a plain `\\x1b[3Xm` below that. Counting\n * only the truecolor form left the 256-colour case unbalanced, which is exactly\n * the leak `crop` exists to prevent. Parsing SGR parameters instead of matching\n * one spelling also keeps `\\x1b[48;5;31m` (a *background*) from being misread as\n * an open foreground, because the extended-colour arguments are skipped.\n */\nfunction styleBalance(value: string): StyleBalance {\n  const balance: StyleBalance = {\n    fgOpens: 0,\n    fgCloses: 0,\n    boldOpens: 0,\n    boldCloses: 0,\n  };\n  for (const match of value.matchAll(/\\x1b\\[([0-9;]*)m/g)) {\n    // `\\x1b[m` is the parameterless spelling of a full reset.\n    const params = match[1].length > 0 ? match[1].split(\";\") : [\"0\"];\n    for (let index = 0; index < params.length; index += 1) {\n      const code = Number(params[index]);\n      // 38/48/58 select an extended foreground/background/underline colour and\n      // carry their own arguments: `5;N` (256 colour) or `2;R;G;B` (truecolor).\n      if (code === 38 || code === 48 || code === 58) {\n        const form = params[index + 1];\n        index += form === \"5\" ? 2 : form === \"2\" ? 4 : 1;\n        if (code === 38) balance.fgOpens += 1;\n        continue;\n      }\n      if ((code >= 30 && code <= 37) || (code >= 90 && code <= 97))\n        balance.fgOpens += 1;\n      else if (code === 39) balance.fgCloses += 1;\n      else if (code === 1) balance.boldOpens += 1;\n      else if (code === 22) balance.boldCloses += 1;\n      else if (code === 0) {\n        balance.fgCloses = balance.fgOpens;\n        balance.boldCloses = balance.boldOpens;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return balance;\n}\n\nexport function crop(value: string, width: number): string {\n  const clipped = truncateToWidth(value, Math.max(1, width), \"…\");\n  // If nothing was truncated, leave the caller's string exactly as is.\n  if (clipped === value) return clipped;\n  const ellipsisIndex = clipped.lastIndexOf(\"…\");\n  if (ellipsisIndex < 0) return clipped;\n  // Strip the reset that truncateToWidth placed immediately before the\n  // ellipsis and any codes it preserved/added after the ellipsis. The suffix\n  // (e.g. a status/runtime marker) starts at the first non-ANSI char after\n  // the trailing reset run.\n  const beforeReset = clipped\n    .slice(0, ellipsisIndex)\n    .replace(/(?:\\x1b\\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z])+$/, \"\");\n  const afterEllipsis = clipped\n    .slice(ellipsisIndex + 1)\n    .replace(/^(?:\\x1b\\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z])+/, \"\");\n  const prefix = `${beforeReset}…`;\n  const { fgOpens, fgCloses, boldOpens, boldCloses } = styleBalance(prefix);\n  const closes: string[] = [];\n  if (boldOpens > boldCloses) closes.push(\"\\x1b[22m\");\n  if (fgOpens > fgCloses) closes.push(\"\\x1b[39m\");\n  return `${prefix}${closes.join(\"\")}${afterEllipsis}`;\n}\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/footer.ts\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { visibleWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\nimport { footerTier, type Layout } from \"../shared/layout.ts\";\nimport {\n  renderSegment,\n  STATUS_SEPARATOR,\n  statusSeparator,\n  type Tone,\n} from \"../shared/ui-theme.ts\";\nimport { compactCwd } from \"./cwd.ts\";\nimport { crop } from \"./layout.ts\";\nimport type { AuroraUiState } from \"./state.ts\";\nimport { pillExtraCells, renderPill } from \"./tile.ts\";\nimport { thinkingLabel, thinkingTone } from \"./thinking.ts\";\n\n/**\n * The footer is the one permanent status surface, and it is one line.\n *\n * Everything it reports comes out of runtime state that some other extension\n * already publishes on the Aurora bus or through `getExtensionStatuses()`.\n * Nothing here starts a process, probes git or the LSP, asks a provider or\n * touches the file system: `render` is called on every frame, so any of that\n * would be paid for continuously and for nothing.\n */\nexport interface FooterInput {\n  state: AuroraUiState;\n  statuses: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;\n  contextPercent: number | null;\n  /** Captured from ExtensionContext at session start; never read from render I/O. */\n  cwd?: string;\n  homeDirectory?: string;\n  /** True while a dashboard surface (auto/compact/expanded) is active: it owns\n   * routine verification reporting, so the footer stops duplicating a plain\n   * \"verified\". Failed/stale checks stay critical footer risks regardless. */\n  dashboardVisible?: boolean;\n}\n\nconst CONTEXT_WARNING_PERCENT = 70;\nconst CONTEXT_CRITICAL_PERCENT = 90;\nconst MODEL_MAX_COLUMNS = 32;\nconst FOLDER_MAX_COLUMNS: Record<Layout, number> = {\n  wide: 34,\n  comfortable: 24,\n  standard: 14,\n  compact: 10,\n};\n\n/**\n * Where a segment sits on the line. Reading order is fixed and independent of\n * how hard the segment fights to stay: risk is loud, but it belongs at the end\n * where a scan finishes, not wedged between the model and the thinking level.\n * A plain object rather than a `const enum` — the test loader transpiles\n * without type information.\n */\nconst Slot = {\n  workflow: 0,\n  model: 1,\n  thinking: 2,\n  folder: 3,\n  context: 4,\n  verification: 5,\n  risk: 6,\n} as const;\n\ntype Slot = (typeof Slot)[keyof typeof Slot];\n\n/**\n * What gets dropped first when the line is too long — lowest survives longest.\n * The workflow is last man standing. Everything that reports an actual problem\n * outranks the model name: at the widths where this matters the user knows\n * which model they picked, and does not yet know their language server died.\n */\nconst Priority = {\n  workflow: 0,\n  yolo: 1,\n  recovery: 2,\n  failedVerification: 3,\n  exhaustedContext: 4,\n  lsp: 5,\n  folder: 6,\n  model: 7,\n  verification: 8,\n  thinking: 9,\n  context: 10,\n} as const;\n\ntype Priority = (typeof Priority)[keyof typeof Priority];\n\ninterface Segment {\n  slot: Slot;\n  priority: Priority;\n  text: string;\n  tone: Tone;\n  bold?: boolean;\n  /**\n   * A segment that reports a risk or a failure. It ignores the width tier,\n   * because a footer that hides YOLO or a failed verification to save four\n   * columns is worse than one that has to drop something else.\n   */\n  critical?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** Which routine segments each width tier may show at all. */\nconst ROUTINE_TIERS: Record<Layout, ReadonlySet<Slot>> = {\n  wide: new Set([\n    Slot.workflow,\n    Slot.model,\n    Slot.thinking,\n    Slot.folder,\n    Slot.context,\n    Slot.verification,\n  ]),\n  comfortable: new Set([\n    Slot.workflow,\n    Slot.model,\n    Slot.thinking,\n    Slot.folder,\n    Slot.context,\n    Slot.verification,\n  ]),\n  standard: new Set([Slot.workflow, Slot.model, Slot.thinking, Slot.folder]),\n  compact: new Set([Slot.workflow, Slot.model, Slot.folder]),\n};\n\nfunction verificationState(input: FooterInput): string | null {\n  const value = input.statuses.get(\"verification\");\n  return value ? value.replace(/^Verify:\\s*/, \"\") : null;\n}\n\nfunction verificationTone(state: string): Tone {\n  if (state === \"verified\") return \"success\";\n  if (state === \"unchanged\") return \"muted\";\n  if (state === \"checks_failed\") return \"error\";\n  return \"warning\";\n}\n\n/** Only an unproven or failing workspace is worth defending space for. */\nfunction verificationNeedsAttention(state: string | null): boolean {\n  return state !== null && state !== \"unchanged\" && state !== \"verified\";\n}\n\nfunction lspState(input: FooterInput): string {\n  return input.statuses.get(\"lsp\") ?? input.state.lsp.state ?? \"—\";\n}\n\n/**\n * A working, idle or disabled language server is not news, so it stays off the\n * line entirely. Only a degraded one is, and it claims space at any width.\n *\n * `extensions/lsp/status.ts` publishes a closed set: `aus`, `leerlauf`,\n * `eingeschränkt` and `${n} aktiv`. Matching the one bad value is what keeps\n * `3 aktiv` — a perfectly healthy server count — out of the warning slot.\n */\nfunction lspNeedsAttention(state: string): boolean {\n  return state === \"eingeschränkt\";\n}\n\nfunction collectSegments(input: FooterInput, width: number): Segment[] {\n  const tier = footerTier(width);\n  const segments: Segment[] = [\n    {\n      slot: Slot.workflow,\n      priority: Priority.workflow,\n      text: input.state.workflow.label,\n      tone: \"accent\",\n      bold: true,\n    },\n    {\n      slot: Slot.model,\n      priority: Priority.model,\n      // The real runtime model id. Prettifying it into a marketing name would\n      // mean inventing a mapping the runtime never gave us.\n      text: crop(input.state.model.id ?? \"kein Modell\", MODEL_MAX_COLUMNS),\n      tone: \"text\",\n    },\n  ];\n\n  if (input.state.model.thinking) {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.thinking,\n      priority: Priority.thinking,\n      text: `Denken ${thinkingLabel(input.state.model.thinking)}`,\n      tone: thinkingTone(input.state.model.thinking),\n      bold: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (input.cwd) {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.folder,\n      priority: Priority.folder,\n      text: compactCwd(\n        input.cwd,\n        FOLDER_MAX_COLUMNS[tier],\n        input.homeDirectory,\n      ),\n      tone: \"muted\",\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (input.contextPercent !== null) {\n    // Colour and layout priority are separate judgements. A filling context is\n    // worth a warning colour long before it is worth taking a narrow line's\n    // space away from the workflow — only a nearly exhausted one is urgent\n    // enough to outrank the tier it would normally be filtered out by.\n    const exhausted = input.contextPercent >= CONTEXT_CRITICAL_PERCENT;\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.context,\n      priority: exhausted ? Priority.exhaustedContext : Priority.context,\n      text: `Kontext ${Math.round(input.contextPercent)}%`,\n      tone: exhausted\n        ? \"error\"\n        : input.contextPercent >= CONTEXT_WARNING_PERCENT\n          ? \"warning\"\n          : \"muted\",\n      critical: exhausted,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const verification = verificationState(input);\n  if (verification !== null) {\n    const attention = verificationNeedsAttention(verification);\n    // Ownership rule: a successful check belongs to the visible dashboard\n    // (or the inspector when auto mode shows nothing right now); the footer\n    // only carries verification that demands attention.\n    const ownedByDashboard = input.dashboardVisible === true && !attention;\n    if (!ownedByDashboard) {\n      segments.push({\n        slot: Slot.verification,\n        priority: attention ? Priority.failedVerification : Priority.verification,\n        text: `${verification === \"verified\" ? \"✓\" : attention ? \"⚠\" : \"\"} ${verification}`.trim(),\n        tone: verificationTone(verification),\n        // Without a dashboard surface owning the verdict, the routine success\n        // stays visible in the footer — but it is not a risk: it keeps normal\n        // priority and yields to real risks on narrow lines like any metadata.\n        critical: attention,\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const lsp = lspState(input);\n  if (lspNeedsAttention(lsp)) {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.risk,\n      priority: Priority.lsp,\n      text: `⚠ LSP ${lsp}`,\n      tone: \"error\",\n      critical: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (input.state.permissions.level === \"yolo\") {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.risk,\n      priority: Priority.yolo,\n      text: \"⚠ YOLO\",\n      tone: \"error\",\n      bold: true,\n      critical: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const recovery = input.statuses.get(\"recovery\");\n  if (recovery) {\n    // Eine offene Recovery-Sperre ist eine aktive Schreibgrenze und bleibt\n    // deshalb unabhängig von der Breite sichtbar.\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.risk,\n      priority: Priority.recovery,\n      text: recovery,\n      tone: \"error\",\n      bold: true,\n      critical: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  return segments;\n}\n\n/**\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/tile.ts\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { visibleWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\nimport type { Tone } from \"../shared/ui-theme.ts\";\nimport { crop } from \"./layout.ts\";\n\n/**\n * The only background fills Aurora ever paints. Pi's public `Theme.bg` accepts\n * exactly these eight tokens, so tiles and pills stay correct in every theme\n * (including `light`) instead of hardcoding ANSI colours. The type is derived\n * from the API itself: `ThemeBg` is not exported by the package entry.\n */\nexport type TileFill = Parameters<Theme[\"bg\"]>[0];\n\nexport interface TileInput {\n  title: string;\n  lines: readonly string[];\n  badge?: string;\n  tone?: \"accent\" | \"muted\" | \"warning\" | \"error\" | \"success\";\n  /** Card background. Omit it and the tile degrades to the flat panel look. */\n  fill?: TileFill;\n}\n\n/** The neutral card surface every tile uses unless a status owns its fill. */\nexport const NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL: TileFill = \"toolPendingBg\";\n\n/**\n * Maps a status tone to the card fill that carries it. Only success and error\n * have dedicated background tokens; everything else sits on the neutral card.\n */\nexport function statusFill(tone: TileInput[\"tone\"]): TileFill {\n  if (tone === \"success\") return \"toolSuccessBg\";\n  if (tone === \"error\") return \"toolErrorBg\";\n  return NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL;\n}\n\n/**\n * A status chip. Loud tones get a filled background (or inverse, since Pi has\n * no warning background token); routine tones stay flat text so a bar of many\n * segments does not turn into a wall of colour.\n */\nexport function renderPill(theme: Theme, text: string, tone: Tone): string {\n  switch (tone) {\n    case \"accent\":\n      return theme.bg(\n        \"selectedBg\",\n        ` ${theme.fg(\"accent\", theme.bold(text))} `,\n      );\n    case \"success\":\n      return theme.bg(\"toolSuccessBg\", ` ${theme.fg(\"success\", text)} `);\n    case \"error\":\n      return theme.bg(\n        \"toolErrorBg\",\n        ` ${theme.fg(\"error\", theme.bold(text))} `,\n      );\n    case \"warning\":\n      return theme.inverse(` ${theme.fg(\"warning\", text)} `);\n    default:\n      return theme.fg(tone, text);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Terminal cells a pill occupies: its text plus one padding cell each side. */\nexport function pillExtraCells(tone: Tone): number {\n  return tone === \"accent\" ||\n    tone === \"success\" ||\n    tone === \"error\" ||\n    tone === \"warning\"\n    ? 2\n    : 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * A labelled field row: muted uppercase label, then the value. Callers style\n * the value themselves; the tile crops the finished row to its inner width.\n */\nexport function renderField(\n  theme: Theme,\n  label: string,\n  value: string,\n): string {\n  return `${theme.fg(\"muted\", theme.bold(label.toUpperCase()))}  ${value}`;\n}\n\nfunction padFilled(\n  theme: Theme,\n  value: string,\n  width: number,\n  fill: TileFill,\n): string {\n  // crop closes any fg/bold left open by the truncation, so the tile fill is\n  // not punctured by a stray full reset and no colour bleeds past this row\n  // into the frame or a neighbouring tile.\n  const clipped = crop(value, width);\n  const padding = \" \".repeat(Math.max(0, width - visibleWidth(clipped)));\n  return `${theme.bg(fill, clipped)}${theme.bg(fill, padding)}`;\n}\n\n/** One empty, filled card row — used to equalise tile heights in a grid. */\nexport function tileBlankRow(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  fill?: TileFill,\n): string {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  if (!fill || available < 18) return \"\";\n  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, available - 4);\n  const border = (value: string) => theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", value);\n  return `${border(\"│ \")}${padFilled(theme, \"\", innerWidth, fill)}${border(\" │\")}`;\n}\n\n/** Card rows: two frame rows plus one row per content line. */\nexport function tileHeight(input: TileInput): number {\n  return input.lines.length + 2;\n}\n\n/**\n * A filled card: the frame shell, but the title row and every content row are\n * padded to the full inner width and painted with `theme.bg`, so the card\n * reads as one solid surface. The < 18 column fallback drops the frame\n * entirely and preserves the content, as the old panel shell did.\n */\nexport function renderTile(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  input: TileInput,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const title = theme.fg(input.tone ?? \"accent\", theme.bold(input.title));\n  const badge = input.badge\n    ? ` ${renderPill(theme, input.badge, input.tone ?? \"muted\")}`\n    : \"\";\n\n  if (available < 18) {\n    return [\n      crop(`${title}${badge}`, available),\n      ...input.lines.map((line) => crop(line, available)),\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, available - 4);\n  const border = (value: string) => theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", value);\n\n  if (!input.fill) {\n    // Unfilled tiles keep the classic framed look without a background.\n    const heading = crop(`${title}${badge}`, innerWidth);\n    const fillDashes = \"─\".repeat(\n      Math.max(0, innerWidth - visibleWidth(heading)),\n    );\n    const row = (value: string) => {\n      const clipped = crop(value, innerWidth);\n      return `${border(\"│ \")}${clipped}${\" \".repeat(\n        Math.max(0, innerWidth - visibleWidth(clipped)),\n      )}${border(\" │\")}`;\n    };\n    return [\n      `${border(\"╭─\")}${heading}${border(`${fillDashes}─╮`)}`,\n      ...input.lines.map(row),\n      border(`╰${\"─\".repeat(Math.max(1, available - 2))}╯`),\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const fill = input.fill;\n  const heading = padFilled(theme, `${title}${badge}`, innerWidth, fill);\n  const row = (value: string) =>\n    `${border(\"│ \")}${padFilled(theme, value, innerWidth, fill)}${border(\" │\")}`;\n\n  return [\n    `${border(\"╭─\")}${heading}${border(\"─╮\")}`,\n    ...input.lines.map(row),\n    border(`╰${\"─\".repeat(Math.max(1, available - 2))}╯`),\n  ];\n}\n\n/**\n * Lays tiles out as a grid. With `columns: 2` consecutive tiles share a row:\n * both get half the width, the shorter one is padded with blank filled rows.\n * An unpaired last tile spans the full width. Widths under two framed tiles\n * degrade to a vertical stack, and a single column always stacks.\n */\nexport function renderTileGrid(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  tiles: readonly TileInput[],\n  columns: 1 | 2 = 1,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  // Two framed tiles side by side need 2 × 18 columns plus one gap column.\n  const framedPairFits = available >= 37;\n  if (columns !== 2 || !framedPairFits) {\n    return tiles.flatMap((tile) => renderTile(theme, available, tile));\n  }\n\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  const columnWidth = Math.floor((available - 1) / 2);\n  for (let index = 0; index < tiles.length; index += 2) {\n    const left = tiles[index]!;\n    const right = tiles[index + 1];\n    if (!right) {\n      lines.push(...renderTile(theme, available, left));\n      continue;\n    }\n    const leftLines = renderTile(theme, columnWidth, left);\n    const rightLines = renderTile(theme, columnWidth, right);\n    const height = Math.max(leftLines.length, rightLines.length);\n    for (let row = 0; row < height; row += 1) {\n      const leftLine =\n        leftLines[row] ?? tileBlankRow(theme, columnWidth, left.fill);\n      const rightLine =\n        rightLines[row] ?? tileBlankRow(theme, columnWidth, right.fill);\n      lines.push(`${leftLine} ${rightLine}`);\n    }\n  }\n  return lines;\n}\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/tool-renderers.ts\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { visibleWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\nimport {\n  footerTier,\n  LAYOUT_COLUMNS,\n  type Layout,\n} from \"../shared/layout.ts\";\nimport { ellipsizeMiddle, toWorkspaceRelative } from \"../shared/paths.ts\";\nimport { crop } from \"./layout.ts\";\nimport {\n  NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL,\n  renderTile,\n  renderTileGrid,\n  statusFill,\n  tileHeight,\n  type TileInput,\n} from \"./tile.ts\";\nimport type {\n  CurrentWorkViewModel,\n  SubagentBranchInfo,\n  TaskPhase,\n  TaskViewModel,\n  VerificationViewModel,\n} from \"./task-view-model.ts\";\n\n/** The compact running marker shared by tool rows. */\nexport const RUNNING_GLYPH = \"◌\";\n\n/**\n * No further progress (e.g. bash producing no new output) is reported in two\n * neutral stages — informational, not an alarm: plenty of legitimate commands\n * (sleep, slow installs) go quiet for a while. Only real failures or explicit\n * attention events ever get warning/error tone.\n */\nconst QUIET_STILL_THRESHOLD_MS = 15_000;\nconst QUIET_INFO_THRESHOLD_MS = 30_000;\n\n/** Presentation-only categories; tool execution remains entirely in Pi core. */\nexport type ActivityToolKind =\n  | \"read\"\n  | \"search\"\n  | \"edit\"\n  | \"bash\"\n  | \"lsp\"\n  | \"test\"\n  | \"verification\"\n  | \"subagent\"\n  | \"wait\"\n  | \"web\"\n  | \"generic\";\n\nexport type ActivityToolTone = \"running\" | \"warning\" | \"error\";\n\nexport interface ActiveToolView {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  kind?: ActivityToolKind;\n  target?: string;\n  startedAt: number;\n  /** Timestamp of the last observed progress (e.g. new bash output). */\n  lastUpdateAt?: number;\n  tone?: ActivityToolTone;\n}\n\n/**\n * A subagent the current turn started. Aurora keeps no independent lifecycle:\n * foreground entries are derived from the actual tool call, async entries from\n * the package's start/complete events, and attention only from its control\n * event.\n */\nexport interface SubagentInfo {\n  agent: string;\n  phase?: string;\n  label?: string;\n  runId?: string;\n  status: \"running\" | \"paused\" | \"needs_attention\" | \"queued\";\n}\n\ninterface ToolPresentation {\n  glyph: string;\n  label: string;\n}\n\nconst PRESENTATIONS: Record<ActivityToolKind, ToolPresentation> = {\n  read: { glyph: \"◌\", label: \"Lesen\" },\n  search: { glyph: \"⌕\", label: \"Suchen\" },\n  edit: { glyph: \"✎\", label: \"Bearbeiten\" },\n  bash: { glyph: \"›\", label: \"Shell\" },\n  lsp: { glyph: \"◇\", label: \"LSP\" },\n  test: { glyph: \"▹\", label: \"Testen\" },\n  verification: { glyph: \"✓\", label: \"Prüfen\" },\n  subagent: { glyph: \"◉\", label: \"Subagent\" },\n  wait: { glyph: \"⋯\", label: \"Warten\" },\n  web: { glyph: \"◎\", label: \"Web\" },\n  generic: { glyph: RUNNING_GLYPH, label: \"Werkzeug\" },\n};\n\nconst LSP_TOOLS = new Set([\n  \"lsp_diagnostics\",\n  \"lsp_definition\",\n  \"lsp_references\",\n  \"lsp_hover\",\n  \"lsp_workspace_symbols\",\n]);\n\nfunction record(value: unknown): Record<string, unknown> | undefined {\n  return value && typeof value === \"object\" && !Array.isArray(value)\n    ? (value as Record<string, unknown>)\n    : undefined;\n}\n\nfunction firstString(\n  args: unknown,\n  candidates: readonly string[],\n): string | undefined {\n  const values = record(args);\n  if (!values) return undefined;\n  for (const key of candidates) {\n    const value = values[key];\n    if (typeof value === \"string\" && value.trim()) return value.trim();\n  }\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction numberValue(args: unknown, key: string): number | undefined {\n  const value = record(args)?.[key];\n  return typeof value === \"number\" && Number.isFinite(value)\n    ? value\n    : undefined;\n}\n\nfunction quote(value: string): string {\n  return `\"${value.replaceAll(\"\\\\\", \"\\\\\\\\\").replaceAll('\"', '\\\\\"')}\"`;\n}\n\nfunction normalizedCommand(args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  return firstString(args, [\"command\"])?.replace(/\\s+/g, \" \");\n}\n\n/**\n * Match only a command position (or a shell chain boundary), not arbitrary\n * output text such as `echo jest`. The command is never executed or parsed by\n * a shell; this only chooses a presentation label for the real bash event.\n */\nfunction commandStarts(command: string, expression: string): boolean {\n  return new RegExp(`(?:^|(?:&&|\\\\|\\\\||;|\\\\n)\\\\s*)${expression}`, \"i\").test(\n    command,\n  );\n}\n\nfunction isVerificationCommand(command: string): boolean {\n  const packageManager = \"(?:npm|pnpm|yarn|bun)\";\n  return (\n    commandStarts(\n      command,\n      `${packageManager}(?:\\\\s+--[^\\\\s]+(?:\\\\s+[^\\\\s]+)?)?\\\\s+run\\\\s+verify(?:\\\\s|$)`,\n    ) || commandStarts(command, `${packageManager}\\\\s+verify(?:\\\\s|$)`)\n  );\n}\n\nfunction isTestCommand(command: string): boolean {\n  const packageManager = \"(?:npm|pnpm|yarn|bun)\";\n  return (\n    commandStarts(\n      command,\n      `${packageManager}(?:\\\\s+--[^\\\\s]+(?:\\\\s+[^\\\\s]+)?)?\\\\s+(?:run\\\\s+)?test(?:\\\\s|$)`,\n    ) ||\n    commandStarts(\n      command,\n      \"(?:npx|pnpx|yarn\\\\s+dlx|bunx\\\\s+)?(?:vitest|jest|pytest)(?:\\\\s|$)\",\n    ) ||\n    commandStarts(command, \"cargo\\\\s+test(?:\\\\s|$)\") ||\n    commandStarts(command, \"go\\\\s+test(?:\\\\s|$)\")\n  );\n}\n\n/** Classify only names and arguments that the running Pi runtime actually emits. */\nexport function classifyTool(\n  toolName: string,\n  args: unknown,\n): ActivityToolKind {\n  const name = toolName.toLowerCase();\n  switch (name) {\n    case \"read\":\n      return \"read\";\n    case \"grep\":\n    case \"find\":\n      return \"search\";\n    case \"edit\":\n    case \"write\":\n      return \"edit\";\n    case \"verify\":\n    case \"project_check\":\n      return \"verification\";\n    case \"subagent\":\n      return \"subagent\";\n    case \"wait\":\n      return \"wait\";\n    case \"web\":\n    case \"web__run\":\n      return \"web\";\n    case \"bash\": {\n      const command = normalizedCommand(args);\n      if (!command) return \"bash\";\n      if (isVerificationCommand(command)) return \"verification\";\n      return isTestCommand(command) ? \"test\" : \"bash\";\n    }\n    default:\n      return LSP_TOOLS.has(name) ? \"lsp\" : \"generic\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction pathWithPosition(cwd: string, args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const path = firstString(args, [\"path\"]);\n  if (!path) return undefined;\n  const relPath = toWorkspaceRelative(cwd, path);\n  const line = numberValue(args, \"line\");\n  const character = numberValue(args, \"character\");\n  if (line === undefined || character === undefined) return relPath;\n  return `${relPath}:${line + 1}:${character + 1}`;\n}\n\nfunction lspTarget(\n  cwd: string,\n  toolName: string,\n  args: unknown,\n): string | undefined {\n  switch (toolName.toLowerCase()) {\n    case \"lsp_diagnostics\": {\n      const path = firstString(args, [\"path\"]);\n      return `Diagnosen · ${path ? toWorkspaceRelative(cwd, path) : \"\"}`.trim();\n    }\n    case \"lsp_definition\":\n      return `Definition · ${pathWithPosition(cwd, args) ?? \"\"}`.trim();\n    case \"lsp_references\":\n      return `Referenzen · ${pathWithPosition(cwd, args) ?? \"\"}`.trim();\n    case \"lsp_hover\":\n      return `Info · ${pathWithPosition(cwd, args) ?? \"\"}`.trim();\n    case \"lsp_workspace_symbols\": {\n      const query = firstString(args, [\"query\"]);\n      return query\n        ? `Workspace-Symbole · ${quote(query)}`\n        : \"Workspace-Symbole\";\n    }\n    default:\n      return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction agentNames(value: unknown): string[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];\n  return value.flatMap((item) => {\n    const entry = record(item);\n    if (!entry) return [];\n    const agent = typeof entry.agent === \"string\" ? entry.agent : undefined;\n    return [\n      ...(agent ? [agent] : []),\n      ...agentNames(entry.tasks),\n      ...agentNames(entry.parallel),\n    ];\n  });\n}\n\nfunction subagentTarget(args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const input = record(args);\n  if (!input) return undefined;\n  if (typeof input.action === \"string\") return input.action;\n  if (typeof input.agent === \"string\") return input.agent;\n  const agents = [...agentNames(input.tasks), ...agentNames(input.chain)];\n  const unique = [...new Set(agents)];\n  if (unique.length === 1) return unique[0];\n  if (unique.length > 1) return `${unique.length} agents`;\n  if (Array.isArray(input.tasks)) return `parallel · ${input.tasks.length}`;\n  if (Array.isArray(input.chain)) return `chain · ${input.chain.length}`;\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction verificationTarget(args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const input = record(args);\n  if (!input) return undefined;\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/startscreen.ts\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { truncateToWidth, visibleWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\nimport { layoutForSize } from \"../shared/layout.ts\";\nimport { compactCwd } from \"./cwd.ts\";\nimport { renderField, renderPill, renderTile } from \"./tile.ts\";\nimport { renderThinkingLabel } from \"./thinking.ts\";\n\nexport interface StartscreenInput {\n  width: number;\n  rows: number;\n  workflow: string;\n  model?: string;\n  thinking?: string;\n  cwd: string;\n  homeDirectory?: string;\n}\n\n/** The welcome window never claims the full terminal — it is a dialog. */\nconst WELCOME_TILE_MAX_COLUMNS = 64;\n\nfunction center(value: string, width: number): string {\n  const clipped = truncateToWidth(value, Math.max(1, width), \"…\");\n  return `${\" \".repeat(Math.max(0, Math.floor((width - visibleWidth(clipped)) / 2)))}${clipped}`;\n}\n\nfunction separator(theme: Theme): string {\n  return theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", \" │ \");\n}\n\nfunction metadata(theme: Theme, input: StartscreenInput, width: number): string {\n  const model = truncateToWidth(input.model ?? \"kein Modell\", 28, \"…\");\n  const values = [\n    theme.fg(\"accent\", theme.bold(input.workflow.toUpperCase())),\n    theme.fg(\"text\", model),\n  ];\n  if (input.thinking) values.push(renderThinkingLabel(theme, input.thinking));\n  return center(values.join(separator(theme)), width);\n}\n\n/** Shortcut chips with their description, wrapped to the tile's inner width. */\nfunction shortcutRows(theme: Theme, innerWidth: number): string[] {\n  const shortcuts: Array<[key: string, label: string]> = [\n    [\"Shift+Tab\", \"Workflow\"],\n    [\"Super+M\", \"Modell\"],\n    [\"Super+D\", \"Denken\"],\n    [\"Super+Q\", \"Befehle\"],\n    [\"Super+S\", \"Rollen\"],\n  ];\n  const rendered = shortcuts.map(\n    ([key, label]) =>\n      `${renderPill(theme, key, \"accent\")} ${theme.fg(\"dim\", label)}`,\n  );\n  const rows: string[] = [];\n  let current = \"\";\n  for (const chip of rendered) {\n    const candidate = current ? `${current}  ${chip}` : chip;\n    if (visibleWidth(candidate) > innerWidth && current) {\n      rows.push(current);\n      current = chip;\n    } else {\n      current = candidate;\n    }\n  }\n  if (current) rows.push(current);\n  return rows;\n}\n\n/**\n * A fresh-session welcome only. Its caller owns whether the session is still\n * fresh; this function only formats values that session state already has.\n *\n * From the standard size class on the welcome is a centered dialog card with\n * labelled fields and shortcut chips; compact terminals keep three centered\n * lines so the frame does not eat the little space they have.\n */\nexport function renderStartscreen(\n  theme: Theme,\n  input: StartscreenInput,\n): string[] {\n  const width = Math.max(1, input.width);\n  const layout = layoutForSize(width, input.rows);\n  const cwd = compactCwd(\n    input.cwd,\n    layout === \"compact\" ? 20 : layout === \"standard\" ? 34 : 48,\n    input.homeDirectory,\n  );\n\n  if (layout === \"compact\")\n    return [\n      center(theme.fg(\"accent\", theme.bold(\"PI · AURORA\")), width),\n      metadata(theme, input, width),\n      center(theme.fg(\"muted\", cwd), width),\n    ];\n\n  const tileWidth = Math.min(width, WELCOME_TILE_MAX_COLUMNS);\n  const model = truncateToWidth(input.model ?? \"kein Modell\", 28, \"…\");\n  const tile = renderTile(theme, tileWidth, {\n    title: \"PI · AURORA\",\n    tone: \"accent\",\n    fill: \"toolPendingBg\",\n    lines: [\n      renderField(\n        theme,\n        \"Workflow\",\n        theme.fg(\"accent\", theme.bold(input.workflow.toUpperCase())),\n      ),\n      renderField(theme, \"Modell\", theme.fg(\"text\", model)),\n      ...(input.thinking\n        ? [renderField(theme, \"Denken\", renderThinkingLabel(theme, input.thinking))]\n        : []),\n      renderField(theme, \"Ordner\", theme.fg(\"muted\", cwd)),\n      \"\",\n      ...shortcutRows(theme, Math.max(1, tileWidth - 4)),\n    ],\n  });\n  return tile.map((row) => center(row, width));\n}\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/state.ts\nimport type { EventBus, ExtensionAPI } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport {\n  FRONTEND_STATE_CHANNELS,\n  type FrontendActivityKind,\n  type FrontendSubagentBranch,\n  type FrontendTask,\n  type FrontendTaskChanges,\n  type FrontendUiPatchEvent,\n  type FrontendUiSnapshotEvent,\n  type FrontendUiState,\n  type FrontendUiStatePatch,\n  type FrontendUiStateRequest,\n  type FrontendVerificationSummary,\n  type FrontendWorkflowPhase,\n} from \"../frontend-protocol/state-contract.ts\";\nimport {\n  isFrontendUiPatchEvent,\n  isFrontendUiSnapshotEvent,\n  isFrontendUiStateRequest,\n  mergeFrontendUiState,\n} from \"../frontend-protocol/state-helpers.ts\";\n\n/**\n * Aurora-Spiegel des versionierten Frontend-Vertrags. isPlanningMode, workflowModeLabel } from \"../shared/workflow-mode.ts\";\nimport type { AuroraUiState, AuroraVerificationSummary } from \"./state.ts\";\nimport {\n  toolPresentation,\n  type ActiveToolView,\n  type SubagentInfo,\n} from \"./tool-renderers.ts\";\nimport type { ReceiptAggregator } from \"./receipts.ts\";\nimport type {\n  CurrentWorkViewModel,\n  SubagentBranchInfo,\n  TaskChangesSummary,\n  TaskPhase,\n  TaskViewModel,\n  VerificationCheck,\n  VerificationCriterion,\n  VerificationViewModel,\n} from \"./task-view-model.ts\";\n\nexport interface TaskProjectionContext {\n  state: AuroraUiState;\n  activeTools: ReadonlyMap<string, ActiveToolView>;\n  subagents: readonly SubagentInfo[];\n  receiptAggregator?: ReceiptAggregator;\n  verificationStatus?: string | null;\n  workspaceChangedSinceVerification?: boolean;\n  currentPlan?: string;\n  userPrompt?: string;\n  contextPercent?: number | null;\n  now?: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Phase describes what is happening right now; verification describes how much\n * the workspace can be trusted (see 02-target-behavior.md phase rules).\n *\n * Precedence:\n * 1. Planning modes keep their explicit planning semantics.\n * 2. Only a real running verification tool claims \"Prüfen\".\n * 3. Active edit/bash/other work stays \"Arbeiten\" even after a failed check.\n * 4. Active thinking means \"understand\" unless planning mode applies.\n * 5. `done` requires idle plus a current successful verification — a stale one\n *    (workspace mutated since the check) never yields `done`.\n * 6. A failed check alone never claims that verification is currently running;\n *    only idle + failed may show it as an invitation to re-check.\n */\nexport interface TaskPhaseInput {\n  mode: WorkflowMode;\n  activityKind: AuroraUiState[\"activity\"][\"kind\"];\n  verificationStatus: string | null | undefined;\n  hasActiveVerificationTool: boolean;\n  verificationIsCurrent?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport function determineTaskPhase({\n  mode,\n  activityKind,\n  verificationStatus,\n  hasActiveVerificationTool,\n  verificationIsCurrent = true,\n}: TaskPhaseInput): { phase: TaskPhase; label: string } {\n  if (isPlanningMode(mode)) {\n    if (activityKind === \"thinking\") {\n      return { phase: \"understand\", label: \"Verstehen\" };\n    }\n    return { phase: \"plan\", label: workflowModeLabel(mode) };\n  }\n\n  if (hasActiveVerificationTool) {\n    return { phase: \"verify\", label: \"Prüfen\" };\n  }\n\n  if (activityKind === \"thinking\") {\n    return { phase: \"understand\", label: \"Analysieren\" };\n  }\n\n  if (activityKind === \"tool\" || activityKind === \"responding\") {\n    return { phase: \"work\", label: \"Arbeiten\" };\n  }\n\n  if (activityKind === \"idle\") {\n    if (verificationStatus === \"verified\" && verificationIsCurrent) {\n      return { phase: \"done\", label: \"Abgeschlossen\" };\n    }\n    if (verificationStatus === \"checks_failed\") {\n      return { phase: \"verify\", label: \"Prüfen\" };\n    }\n    return { phase: \"work\", label: \"Bereit\" };\n  }\n\n  return { phase: \"work\", label: \"Arbeiten\" };\n}\n\nexport function extractTaskTitle(\n  userPrompt?: string,\n  currentPlan?: string,\n  mode?: WorkflowMode,\n): { title: string; goal?: string } {\n  if (currentPlan) {\n    const lines = currentPlan.split(\"\\n\");\n    const heading = lines\n      .find((line) => line.startsWith(\"# \"))\n      ?.replace(/^#\\s*/, \"\")\n      .trim();\n    if (heading) {\n      const goalLine = lines.find(\n        (line) =>\n          line.toLowerCase().includes(\"ziel:\") ||\n          line.toLowerCase().includes(\"goal:\"),\n      );\n      return {\n        title: heading,\n        goal: goalLine\n          ? goalLine.replace(/^[#*\\s-]*ziel:\\s*/i, \"\").trim()\n          : undefined,\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  if (userPrompt) {\n    const trimmed = userPrompt.trim().replace(/^[/#]\\w+\\s*/, \"\");\n    const firstLine = trimmed.split(\"\\n\")[0]?.trim() ?? \"\";\n    if (firstLine.length > 0) {\n      return {\n        title: firstLine.length > 60 ? `${firstLine.slice(0, 57)}…` : firstLine,\n        goal:\n          trimmed.length > firstLine.length\n            ? trimmed.slice(firstLine.length).trim()\n            : undefined,\n      };\n    }\n  }\n\n  return {\n    title: isPlanningMode(mode ?? \"work\") ? \"Planung\" : \"Aktuelle Aufgabe\",\n  };\n}\n\nfunction checksFromVerificationSummary(\n  summary: AuroraVerificationSummary,\n): VerificationCheck[] {\n  const checks: VerificationCheck[] = summary.declaredRequiredIds.map((id) => {\n    const outcome = summary.requiredOutcomes[id];\n    const status: VerificationCheck[\"status\"] =\n      outcome === \"success\"\n        ? \"passed\"\n        : outcome === \"failed\"\n          ? \"failed\"\n          : outcome === \"unavailable\"\n            ? \"unavailable\"\n            : \"pending\";\n    return { id, label: id, status };\n  });\n  for (const id of summary.blockingRecommendedIds) {\n    checks.push({\n      id,\n      label: id,\n      status: \"failed\",\n      detail: \"empfohlene Prüfung blockiert\",\n    });\n  }\n  return checks;\n}\n\nfunction criteriaFromChecks(\n  checks: readonly VerificationCheck[],\n): VerificationCriterion[] {\n  return checks.map((check) => ({\n    label: check.label,\n    status:\n      check.status === \"passed\"\n        ? \"passed\"\n        : check.status === \"failed\"\n          ? \"failed\"\n          : \"pending\",\n    detail: check.detail,\n  }));\n}\n\n/** Verdict from real, upstream-decided signals only — never recomputed from\n * raw outcomes, so Aurora cannot disagree with the one place\n * (`verificationStatus()` in setup-core) that is supposed to decide this. The\n * one exception is a defensive floor: \"verified\" without a single declared\n * required check is a contradiction, not evidence, so it is shown as\n * unverified rather than trusted at face value. */\nfunction verdictFromSummary(\n  summary: AuroraVerificationSummary,\n  isStale: boolean,\n): VerificationViewModel[\"verdict\"] {\n  if (isStale) return \"UNVERIFIED\";\n  if (summary.status === \"verified\") {\n    return summary.declaredRequiredIds.length > 0 ? \"READY\" : \"UNVERIFIED\";\n  }\n  if (summary.status === \"checks_failed\") return \"NOT_READY\";\n  return \"UNVERIFIED\";\n}\n\nfunction projectVerificationFromSummary(\n  summary: AuroraVerificationSummary,\n  isStale: boolean,\n): VerificationViewModel {\n  const checks = checksFromVerificationSummary(summary);\n  const failedOrUnavailable = checks.filter(\n    (c) => c.status === \"failed\" || c.status === \"unavailable\",\n  );\n\n  return {\n    verdict: verdictFromSummary(summary, isStale),\n    criteria: criteriaFromChecks(checks),\n    checks,\n    evidence: failedOrUnavailable.map(\n      (c) =>\n        `${c.label}: ${c.status === \"failed\" ? \"fehlgeschlagen\" : \"nicht verfügbar\"}`,\n    ),\n    testsPassed: checks.filter((c) => c.status === \"passed\").length,\n    testsTotal: summary.declaredRequiredIds.length,\n    blockers: checks\n      .filter((c) => c.status === \"failed\")\n      .map((c) => `Pflichtprüfung \"${c.label}\" ist fehlgeschlagen.`),\n  };\n}\n\n/** Coarse fallback for when no extension has published a structured\n * verification summary yet (untrusted project, verification status disabled,\n * or Aurora activated before the first `agent_settled`). Degrades to the\n * plain status string instead of showing nothing. */\nfunction projectVerificationFallback(\n  status: string | null | undefined,\n  isStale: boolean,\n): VerificationViewModel | undefined {\n  if (!status && !isStale) return undefined;\n\n  const normalized = status ? status.replace(/^Verify:\\s*/, \"\") : null;\n  const isVerified = normalized === \"verified\";\n  const isFailed = normalized === \"checks_failed\";\n  const verdict = isStale\n    ? \"UNVERIFIED\"\n    : isVerified\n      ? \"READY\"\n      : isFailed\n        ? \"NOT_READY\"\n        : \"UNVERIFIED\";\n\n  return {\n    verdict,\n    criteria: [\n      {\n        label: \"Änderungen implementiert\",\n        status: isVerified ? \"passed\" : \"pending\",\n      },\n      {\n        label: \"Tests & Prüfungen bestanden\",\n        status: isVerified ? \"passed\" : isFailed ? \"failed\" : \"pending\",\n      },\n    ],\n    checks: [],\n    evidence: [],\n    blockers: isFailed\n      ? [\"Mindestens eine Verifikationsprüfung ist fehlgeschlagen.\"]\n      : [],\n  };\n}\n\n/** The one staleness definition for both verdict and phase: an earlier check\n * no longer describes the workspace once an edit ran or is running, or once\n * the caller recorded a mutation after the last completed check. */\nexport function verificationIsStale(\n  activeTools: ReadonlyMap<string, ActiveToolView>,\n  workspaceChangedSinceVerification: boolean,\n): boolean {\n  return (\n    workspaceChangedSinceVerification ||\n    [...activeTools.values()].some(\n      (t) => t.kind === \"verification\" || t.kind === \"edit\",\n    )\n  );\n}\n\nexport function projectVerificationState(\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/cwd.ts\nimport { ellipsizeMiddle } from \"../shared/paths.ts\";\n\n/**\n * Pure, terminal-cell-safe display form for the session cwd.\n *\n * The caller supplies both paths from session state, so this helper never\n * resolves paths or touches the filesystem. It favors a home-relative full\n * path first, then an ellipsized path that retains the final directory.\n */\nexport function compactCwd(\n  cwd: string,\n  maxColumns: number,\n  homeDirectory?: string,\n): string {\n  const home = homeDirectory?.replace(/\\/+$/, \"\");\n  const normalized = cwd || \"/\";\n  const withinHome = Boolean(\n    home && (normalized === home || normalized.startsWith(`${home}/`)),\n  );\n  const display = withinHome\n    ? normalized === home\n      ? \"~\"\n      : `~${normalized.slice(home!.length)}`\n    : normalized;\n\n  return ellipsizeMiddle(display, maxColumns);\n}\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/inspector.ts\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { renderTile } from \"./tile.ts\";\n\nexport interface InspectorSection {\n  title: string;\n  lines: string[];\n  tone?: \"accent\" | \"muted\" | \"warning\" | \"error\" | \"success\";\n}\n\nexport interface InspectorContent {\n  title: string;\n  badge?: string;\n  sections: InspectorSection[];\n  actions?: Array<{ label: string; key?: string }>;\n}\n\nexport function renderInspectorBox(\n  content: InspectorContent,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n): string[] {\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  for (const section of content.sections) {\n    if (section.title) {\n      lines.push(theme.fg(section.tone ?? \"accent\", theme.bold(section.title)));\n    }\n    lines.push(...section.lines.map((line) => `  ${line}`));\n  }\n  if (content.actions && content.actions.length > 0) {\n    const actionText = content.actions\n      .map((action) => `${action.label}${action.key ? ` · ${action.key}` : \"\"}`)\n      .join(\"   \");\n    lines.push(theme.fg(\"muted\", actionText));\n  }\n  return renderTile(theme, Math.min(width, 76), {\n    title: content.title,\n    badge: content.badge,\n    fill: \"toolPendingBg\",\n    lines,\n  });\n}\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/dev-diagnostics.ts\n/**\n * Development-only render diagnostics for the Aurora activity widget.\n *\n * Activated only when PI_AURORA_DIAG=1 (or \"true\") is set at session start;\n * otherwise every method is a no-op so normal sessions pay nothing. The\n * counters answer the questions from pi-tui-optimization-package/03:\n * how many renders ran, roughly how long they took, which tick interval was\n * active, and how many dashboard rows were emitted.\n *\n * This module reports only; it never changes rendering decisions.\n */\n\nfunction envEnabled(): boolean {\n  const value = process.env.PI_AURORA_DIAG;\n  return value === \"1\" || value === \"true\";\n}\n\nexport interface AuroraDiagOptions {\n  /** Overrides env detection; only used by tests. */\n  enabled?: boolean;\n}\n\nexport interface AuroraDiagSnapshot {\n  renderCount: number;\n  totalRenderMs: number;\n  maxRenderMs: number;\n  lastDashboardRows: number;\n  activeTickIntervalMs: number | null;\n}\n\nclass AuroraDiagnostics {\n  private isEnabled: boolean;\n  private renderCount = 0;\n  private totalRenderMs = 0;\n  private maxRenderMs = 0;\n  private lastDashboardRows = 0;\n  private activeTickIntervalMs: number | null = null;\n\n  constructor(options?: AuroraDiagOptions) {\n    this.isEnabled = options?.enabled ?? envEnabled();\n  }\n\n  get enabled(): boolean {\n    return this.isEnabled;\n  }\n\n  /** Wraps one widget render call; measures wall time around `fn`. */\n  measure<R>(fn: () => R): R {\n    if (!this.isEnabled) return fn();\n    const started = performance.now();\n    try {\n      return fn();\n    } finally {\n      const elapsed = performance.now() - started;\n      this.renderCount += 1;\n      this.totalRenderMs += elapsed;\n      if (elapsed > this.maxRenderMs) this.maxRenderMs = elapsed;\n    }\n  }\n\n  recordDashboardRows(rows: readonly unknown[]): void {\n    if (!this.isEnabled) return;\n    this.lastDashboardRows = rows.length;\n  }\n\n  recordTickInterval(intervalMs: number | null): void {\n    if (!this.isEnabled) return;\n    this.activeTickIntervalMs = intervalMs;\n  }\n\n  snapshot(): AuroraDiagSnapshot {\n    return {\n      renderCount: this.renderCount,\n      totalRenderMs: Math.round(this.totalRenderMs * 1000) / 1000,\n      maxRenderMs: Math.round(this.maxRenderMs * 1000) / 1000,\n      lastDashboardRows: this.lastDashboardRows,\n      activeTickIntervalMs: this.activeTickIntervalMs,\n    };\n  }\n\n  reset(): void {\n    this.renderCount = 0;\n    this.totalRenderMs = 0;\n    this.maxRenderMs = 0;\n    this.lastDashboardRows = 0;\n    // The tick interval survives resets: it describes configuration, not load.\n  }\n\n  /** One-line summary for manual smoke runs; silent unless enabled. */\n  report(): string | undefined {\n    if (!this.isEnabled) return undefined;\n    const s = this.snapshot();\n    const avg =\n      s.renderCount > 0 ? Math.round((s.totalRenderMs / s.renderCount) * 1000) / 1000 : 0;\n    return (\n      `[aurora-diag] renders=${s.renderCount} total=${s.totalRenderMs}ms ` +\n      `avg=${avg}ms max=${s.maxRenderMs}ms rows=${s.lastDashboardRows} ` +\n      `tick=${s.activeTickIntervalMs ?? \"off\"}ms`\n    );\n  }\n}\n\nexport const auroraDiagnostics = new AuroraDiagnostics();\n\n/** Fresh instance for tests; same semantics as the shared singleton. */\nexport function createAuroraDiagnostics(options?: AuroraDiagOptions): AuroraDiagnostics {\n  return new AuroraDiagnostics(options);\n}\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/receipts.ts\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { crop } from \"./layout.ts\";\nimport type {\n  ReceiptKind,\n  ReceiptStatus,\n  TaskReceipt,\n} from \"./task-view-model.ts\";\n\nexport interface ToolExecutionRecord {\n  id: string;\n  toolName: string;\n  args: unknown;\n  result?: unknown;\n  isError?: boolean;\n  startedAt: number;\n  completedAt?: number;\n}\n\n/** How many recently completed tool records {@link ReceiptAggregator.getRecord}\n * can still resolve. Bounded so a long session cannot grow this without limit;\n * the receipts themselves (and error details in particular) never depend on\n * the record surviving here — they already carry their own summary/errorDetails\n * inline, so an evicted record only loses its optional drill-down target, not\n * the receipt's visible content. */\nconst MAX_RECENT_RECORDS = 20;\n\nexport class ReceiptAggregator {\n  private activeRecords = new Map<string, ToolExecutionRecord>();\n  private completedReceipts: TaskReceipt[] = [];\n  private recentRecords: ToolExecutionRecord[] = [];\n\n  // Aggregated counters\n  private inspectedFiles = new Set<string>();\n  private searchQueries = new Set<string>();\n  private editedFiles = new Set<string>();\n  // Id of the most recent completed record behind each aggregated receipt,\n  // used as that receipt's detailRef — never invented, only ever a real id.\n  private lastRecordId: {\n    investigation?: string;\n    search?: string;\n    edit?: string;\n  } = {};\n\n  recordStart(\n    id: string,\n    toolName: string,\n    args: unknown,\n    startedAt: number,\n  ): void {\n    this.activeRecords.set(id, {\n      id,\n      toolName,\n      args,\n      startedAt,\n    });\n  }\n\n  recordEnd(\n    id: string,\n    result: unknown,\n    isError: boolean,\n    completedAt: number,\n  ): void {\n    const record = this.activeRecords.get(id);\n    if (!record) return;\n    record.result = result;\n    record.isError = isError;\n    record.completedAt = completedAt;\n    this.activeRecords.delete(id);\n    this.recentRecords.push(record);\n    if (this.recentRecords.length > MAX_RECENT_RECORDS) {\n      this.recentRecords.shift();\n    }\n    this.processCompletedRecord(record);\n  }\n\n  /** Resolves a receipt's `detailRef` back to its raw tool record, when it is\n   * still within the recent-records window. */\n  getRecord(id: string): ToolExecutionRecord | undefined {\n    return this.recentRecords.find((record) => record.id === id);\n  }\n\n  reset(): void {\n    this.activeRecords.clear();\n    this.completedReceipts = [];\n    this.recentRecords = [];\n    this.inspectedFiles.clear();\n    this.searchQueries.clear();\n    this.editedFiles.clear();\n    this.lastRecordId = {};\n  }\n\n  private extractPath(args: unknown): string | undefined {\n    if (!args || typeof args !== \"object\") return undefined;\n    const rec = args as Record<string, unknown>;\n    if (typeof rec.path === \"string\") return rec.path;\n    if (typeof rec.filePath === \"string\") return rec.filePath;\n    if (typeof rec.file === \"string\") return rec.file;\n    if (typeof rec.pattern === \"string\") return rec.pattern;\n    if (typeof rec.query === \"string\") return rec.query;\n    return undefined;\n  }\n\n  private processCompletedRecord(record: ToolExecutionRecord): void {\n    const { toolName, args, isError, result } = record;\n    const target = this.extractPath(args);\n\n    if (\n      toolName === \"read\" ||\n      toolName === \"read_file\" ||\n      toolName === \"view_file\" ||\n      toolName.startsWith(\"read_\")\n    ) {\n      if (target) {\n        this.inspectedFiles.add(target);\n        this.lastRecordId.investigation = record.id;\n      }\n    } else if (\n      toolName === \"grep\" ||\n      toolName === \"find\" ||\n      toolName === \"grep_search\" ||\n      toolName === \"find_by_name\" ||\n      toolName.startsWith(\"lsp_\")\n    ) {\n      if (target) {\n        this.searchQueries.add(target);\n        this.lastRecordId.search = record.id;\n      }\n    } else if (\n      toolName === \"edit\" ||\n      toolName === \"write\" ||\n      toolName === \"write_to_file\"\n    ) {\n      if (target) {\n        this.editedFiles.add(target);\n        this.lastRecordId.edit = record.id;\n      }\n    }\n\n    if (isError) {\n      const errorMsg =\n        typeof result === \"string\"\n          ? result\n          : result && typeof result === \"object\" && \"content\" in result\n            ? String((result as { content: unknown }).content)\n            : `Tool ${toolName} fehlgeschlagen.`;\n\n      this.completedReceipts.push({\n        id: record.id,\n        kind: \"generic\",\n        status: \"failed\",\n        title: `${toolName} (Fehler)`,\n        summary: errorMsg.slice(0, 120),\n        errorDetails: errorMsg,\n        startedAt: record.startedAt,\n        completedAt: record.completedAt,\n        detailRef: record.id,\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  getReceipts(): TaskReceipt[] {\n    const receipts: TaskReceipt[] = [];\n\n    // Investigation receipt\n    if (this.inspectedFiles.size > 0) {\n      const count = this.inspectedFiles.size;\n      receipts.push({\n        id: \"receipt-investigation\",\n        kind: \"investigation\",\n        status: \"completed\",\n        title: \"Investigation\",\n        summary: `${count} ${count === 1 ? \"Datei\" : \"Dateien\"} untersucht`,\n        metrics: [`${count} files inspected`],\n        evidenceRefs: [...this.inspectedFiles].slice(0, 5),\n        detailRef: this.lastRecordId.investigation,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Search receipt\n    if (this.searchQueries.size > 0) {\n      const count = this.searchQueries.size;\n      receipts.push({\n        id: \"receipt-search\",\n        kind: \"search\",\n        status: \"completed\",\n        title: \"Suche\",\n        summary: `${count} ${count === 1 ? \"Suchabfrage\" : \"Suchabfragen\"} ausgeführt`,\n        metrics: [`${count} patterns queried`],\n        evidenceRefs: [...this.searchQueries].slice(0, 5),\n        detailRef: this.lastRecordId.search,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Changes receipt\n    if (this.editedFiles.size > 0) {\n      const count = this.editedFiles.size;\n      receipts.push({\n        id: \"receipt-edits\",\n        kind: \"edit\",\n        status: \"completed\",\n        title: \"Änderungen\",\n        // Diff statistics belong to diff-viewer, which is the only component\n        // with the actual before/after snapshots. A receipt must not guess.\n        summary: `${count} ${count === 1 ? \"Datei geändert\" : \"Dateien geändert\"}`,\n        metrics: [`${count} files`],\n        evidenceRefs: [...this.editedFiles].slice(0, 5),\n        detailRef: this.lastRecordId.edit,\n      });\n    }\n\n    // Explicit error receipts\n    receipts.push(...this.completedReceipts);\n\n    return receipts;\n  }\n}\n\nexport function receiptGlyph(kind: ReceiptKind, status: ReceiptStatus): string {\n  if (status === \"failed\") return \"✗\";\n  if (status === \"warning\") return \"⚠\";\n  if (status === \"running\") return \"◌\";\n  switch (kind) {\n    case \"investigation\":\n      return \"✓\";\n    case \"search\":\n      return \"⌕\";\n    case \"edit\":\n      return \"✎\";\n    case \"test\":\n      return \"▹\";\n    case \"verification\":\n      return \"✓\";\n    case \"subagent\":\n      return \"◉\";\n    case \"generic\":\n      return \"✓\";\n  }\n}\n\nexport function renderReceiptLines(\n  receipts: readonly TaskReceipt[],\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  limit = 4,\n): string[] {\n  if (receipts.length === 0) return [];\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  const visible = receipts.slice(-limit);\n\n  for (const receipt of visible) {\n    const glyph = receiptGlyph(receipt.kind, receipt.status);\n    const tone =\n      receipt.status === \"failed\"\n        ? \"error\"\n        : receipt.status === \"warning\"\n          ? \"warning\"\n          : receipt.status === \"running\"\n            ? \"accent\"\n            : \"success\";\n\n    const header = `${theme.fg(tone, glyph)} ${theme.bold(receipt.title)}: ${receipt.summary}`;\n    lines.push(crop(header, width));\n\n    if (receipt.findings && receipt.findings.length > 0) {\n      for (const finding of receipt.findings.slice(0, 2)) {\n        const sevTone =\n          finding.severity === \"HIGH\"\n            ? \"error\"\n            : finding.severity === \"MEDIUM\"\n              ? \"warning\"\n              : \"muted\";\n        const sev = finding.severity ? `[${finding.severity}] ` : \"\";\n        lines.push(\n          crop(`  ${theme.fg(sevTone, sev)}${finding.message}`, width),\n        );\n      }\n    }\n  }\n\n\n### extensions/aurora-ui/inspector-command.ts\nimport type {\n  ExtensionAPI,\n  ExtensionContext,\n} from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { catalogDescription } from \"../shared/command-catalog.ts\";\nimport { collectContextDiagnostics } from \"../setup-core/context-diagnostics.ts\";\nimport { renderInspectorBox, type InspectorContent } from \"./inspector.ts\";\nimport { thinkingLabel } from \"./thinking.ts\";\nimport type { AuroraUiState } from \"./state.ts\";\nimport type {\n  TaskViewModel,\n  VerificationViewModel,\n} from \"./task-view-model.ts\";\n\nexport interface InspectorDeps {\n  getState: () => AuroraUiState | undefined;\n  getTask: () => TaskViewModel | undefined;\n}\n\ninterface InspectorEntry {\n  id:\n    | \"changes\"\n    | \"context\"\n    | \"verification\"\n    | \"models\"\n    | \"reasoning\"\n    | \"diagnostics\";\n  label: string;\n}\n\n// Same priority order the UI rework concept package gives for unifying\n// secondary surfaces behind one inspector shell.\nconst ENTRIES: readonly InspectorEntry[] = [\n  { id: \"changes\", label: \"Changes\" },\n  { id: \"context\", label: \"Kontext\" },\n  { id: \"verification\", label: \"Verification Evidence\" },\n  { id: \"models\", label: \"Modelle\" },\n  { id: \"reasoning\", label: \"Reasoning\" },\n  { id: \"diagnostics\", label: \"Diagnostics\" },\n];\n\nfunction changesContent(state: AuroraUiState): InspectorContent {\n  const changes = state.changes;\n  return {\n    title: \"CHANGES\",\n    badge: changes\n      ? [\n          {\n            title: \"Diff\",\n            lines: [`+${changes.linesAdded} −${changes.linesRemoved}`],\n          },\n          { title: \"Dateien\", lines: changes.files.slice(0, 10) },\n        ]\n      : [\n          {\n            title: \"\",\n            lines: [\"Keine Edit-/Write-Operationen in dieser Session.\"],\n          },\n        ],\n    actions: [{ label: \"Details/Diff\", key: \"/changes\" }],\n  };\n}\n\n/**\n * Deliberately does NOT break the active context down by category\n * (conversation/files/tool results/memory) — that would need a per-category\n * token estimate the runtime cannot provide without guessing, which the UI\n * rework's own leitplanken rule out as fake precision. Only fields the\n * runtime reports exactly (bytes, counts, timestamps) are shown.\n */\nfunction contextContent(\n  diagnostics: ReturnType<typeof collectContextDiagnostics>,\n): InspectorContent {\n  const active = diagnostics.activeContext;\n  const percentText =\n    active?.percent === null || active?.percent === undefined\n      ? \"n/a\"\n      : `${active.percent.toFixed(1)}%`;\n  return {\n    title: \"KONTEXT\",\n    badge: percentText,\n    sections: [\n      {\n        title: \"Fenster\",\n        lines: [\n          `Tokens: ${active?.tokens ?? \"n/a\"} / ${active?.contextWindow ?? \"n/a\"}`,\n          `Anteil: ${percentText}`,\n        ],\n      },\n      {\n        title: \"Bytes (deterministisch)\",\n        lines: [\n          `System-Prompt: ${diagnostics.systemPromptBytes ?? \"n/a\"} bytes`,\n          `Aktive Tool-Schemas: ${diagnostics.schemaBytes} bytes`,\n        ],\n      },\n      {\n        title: \"Compaction\",\n        lines: [\n          `Anzahl: ${diagnostics.compactionTimestamps.length}`,\n          `Zuletzt: ${diagnostics.compactionTimestamps.at(-1) ?? \"keine\"}`,\n        ],\n      },\n      {\n        title: \"Lifetime Usage (kumulativ, kein aktueller Anteil)\",\n        lines: diagnostics.lifetimeUsage\n          ? [];\n  if (checks.length === 0) return [\"keine deklarierten Pflichtprüfungen\"];\n  return checks.map((check) => {\n    const glyph =\n      check.status === \"passed\" ? \"✓\" : check.status === \"failed\" ? \"✗\" : \"○\";\n    return `${glyph} ${check.label} (${check.status})`;\n  });\n}\n\nfunction verificationContent(\n  verification: VerificationViewModel | undefined,\n): InspectorContent {\n  if (!verification) {\n    return {\n      title: \"VERIFICATION EVIDENCE\",\n      sections: [\n        { title: \"\", lines: [\"Keine Verification-Daten für diese Session.\"] },\n      ],\n    };\n  }\n  return {\n    title: \"VERIFICATION EVIDENCE\",\n    badge: verification.verdict,\n    sections: [\n      { title: \"Checks\", lines: verificationChecksLines(verification) },\n      {\n        title: \"Evidence\",\n        lines:\n          verification.evidence && verification.evidence.length > 0\n            ? verification.evidence\n            : [\"keine\"],\n      },\n    ],\n \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\n\n/**\n * The subset of Pi's `ThemeColor` that this repository's chrome uses. Segments are told apart by their\n * foreground color and by the separator their caller places between them; on\n * wide tiers callers may upgrade individual segments to pills via\n * `aurora-ui/tile.ts`, but the default stays a flat, background-free run.\n */\nexport function renderSegment(\n  theme: Theme,\n  text: string,\n  options: { tone?: Tone; icon?: string; bold?: boolean } = {},\n): string {\n  const { tone = \"text\", icon, bold = false } = options;\n  const content = icon ? `${icon} ${text}` : text;\n  return theme.fg(tone, bold ? theme.bold(content) : content);\n}\n\n/**\n * The one separator glyph every status bar (footer, menu hint lines) joins\n * its segments with.
