Two\n * surfaces therefore disagreed about what \"narrow\" meant, and neither boundary\n * was stated anywhere a third surface could find it.\n *\n * This file deliberately imports nothing. `menu-ui.ts` notes that a static\n * `pi-tui` import breaks the jiti test loader, and every menu module reaches\n * these thresholds — keeping the size math dependency-free means importing it\n * can never pull a renderer into a module that must stay loadable.\n */\n\n/**\n * A — compact: single-line entries, no descriptions, no detail area.\n * B — standard: one description line per entry.\n * C — comfortable: two description lines plus a detail area.\n * D — wide: room for the optional extras; no surface may claim it permanently.\n */\nexport type Layout = \"compact\" | \"standard\" | \"comfortable\" | \"wide\";\n\nexport const LAYOUT_COLUMNS = {\n  standard: 52,\n  comfortable: 90,\n  wide: 120,\n} as const;\n\nexport const LAYOUT_ROWS = {\n  standard: 14,\n  comfortable: 28,\n  wide: 30,\n} as const;\n\n/** Below this width an overlay gives up its outer margin to gain content. */\nconst TIGHT_MARGIN_COLUMNS = 36;\n\n/**\n * The size class of a full terminal. Both dimensions must clear a threshold:\n * a 200×10 terminal is compact, because height is what the entry lists and\n * detail areas actually spend.\n */\nexport function layoutForSize(columns: number, rows: number): Layout {\n  if (columns < LAYOUT_COLUMNS.standard || rows < LAYOUT_ROWS.standard)\n    return \"compact\";\n  if (columns >= LAYOUT_COLUMNS.wide && rows >= LAYOUT_ROWS.wide) return \"wide\";\n  if (\n    columns >= LAYOUT_COLUMNS.comfortable &&\n    rows >= LAYOUT_ROWS.comfortable\n  )\n    return \"comfortable\";\n  return \"standard\";\n}\n\n/**\n * The size class of a single-line surface. The footer occupies one row at any\n * height, so only its width can decide how much it may say.\n */\nexport function footerTier(columns: number): Layout {\n  if (columns < LAYOUT_COLUMNS.standard) return \"compact\";\n  if (columns >= LAYOUT_COLUMNS.wide) return \"wide\";\n  if (columns >= LAYOUT_COLUMNS.comfortable) return \"comfortable\";\n  return \"standard\";\n}\n\n/** The outer gap an overlay leaves on every side of the terminal. */\nexport function overlayMargin(columns: number, rows: number): 1 | 2 {\n  return columns < TIGHT_MARGIN_COLUMNS || rows < LAYOUT_ROWS.standard ? 1 : 2;\n}\n\n/**\n * Whether a visible dashboard surface currently owns the routine verification\n * report, so the footer can stand down for it. `auto` spends its second row on\n * live work on small terminals; in that narrow active state no dashboard row\n * carries the verdict and the footer keeps reporting routine success.\n */\nexport function dashboardOwnsVerification(\n  mode: \"auto\" | \"compact\" | \"expanded\" | \"hidden\",\n  columns: number,\n  rows: number,\n  busy: boolean,\n): boolean {\n  if (mode === \"hidden\") return false;\n  if (mode === \"compact\" || mode === \"expanded\") return true;\n  return !(layoutForSize(columns, rows) === \"compact\" && busy);\n}\n\n### extensions/shared/shortcuts.ts\n/**\n * Zentrale Shortcut-Definitionen. Only the truncation stayed behind: it is the\n * one piece that needs `pi-tui` at module scope, and `shared/layout.ts` has to\n * remain import-free so the menu modules can use it under the jiti test loader.\n *\n * `truncateToWidth` wraps its injected ellipsis in full resets\n * (`\\x1b[0m…\\x1b[0m`) and may preserve the original closing codes after it.\n * That punches an unfilled hole into a tile row and can leave the preceding\n * `\\x1b[38;...m` or `\\x1b[1m` unclosed; the unclosed codes then bleed into the\n * next segment, frame, or neighbouring tile. This wrapper removes the reset\n * artifacts, keeps any suffix that still fits, and re-balances the visible\n * prefix by appending only the closes that are actually missing\n * (`\\x1b[39m` for foreground, `\\x1b[22m` for bold).\n */\n\ninterface StyleBalance {\n  fgOpens: number;\n  fgCloses: number;\n  boldOpens: number;\n  boldCloses: number;\n}\n\n/**\n * Counts the foreground and bold codes a string leaves open.\n *\n * Pi's theme picks its escape form from the detected colour depth, so the same\n * `theme.fg(...)` call emits `\\x1b[38;2;r;g;bm` on a truecolor terminal,\n * `\\x1b[38;5;Nm` on a 256-colour one and a plain `\\x1b[3Xm` below that. Counting\n * only the truecolor form left the 256-colour case unbalanced, which is exactly\n * the leak `crop` exists to prevent. Parsing SGR parameters instead of matching\n * one spelling also keeps `\\x1b[48;5;31m` (a *background*) from being misread as\n * an open foreground, because the extended-colour arguments are skipped.\n */\nfunction styleBalance(value: string): StyleBalance {\n  const balance: StyleBalance = {\n    fgOpens: 0,\n    fgCloses: 0,\n    boldOpens: 0,\n    boldCloses: 0,\n  };\n  for (const match of value.matchAll(/\\x1b\\[([0-9;]*)m/g)) {\n    // `\\x1b[m` is the parameterless spelling of a full reset.\n    const params = match[1].length > 0 ? match[1].split(\";\") : [\"0\"];\n    for (let index = 0; index < params.length; index += 1) {\n      const code = Number(params[index]);\n      // 38/48/58 select an extended foreground/background/underline colour and\n      // carry their own arguments: `5;N` (256 colour) or `2;R;G;B` (truecolor).\n      if (code === 38 || code === 48 || code === 58) {\n        const form = params[index + 1];\n        index += form === \"5\" ? 2 : form === \"2\" ? 4 : 1;\n        if (code === 38) balance.fgOpens += 1;\n        continue;\n      }\n      if ((code >= 30 && code <= 37) || (code >= 90 && code <= 97))\n        balance.fgOpens += 1;\n      else if (code === 39) balance.fgCloses += 1;\n      else if (code === 1) balance.boldOpens += 1;\n      else if (code === 22) balance.boldCloses += 1;\n      else if (code === 0) {\n        balance.fgCloses = balance.fgOpens;\n        balance.boldCloses = balance.boldOpens;\n      }\n    }\n  }\n  return balance;\n}\n\nexport function crop(value: string, width: number): string {\n  const clipped = truncateToWidth(value, Math.max(1, width), \"…\");\n  // If nothing was truncated, leave the caller's string exactly as is.\n  if (clipped === value) return clipped;\n  const ellipsisIndex = clipped.lastIndexOf(\"…\");\n  if (ellipsisIndex < 0) return clipped;\n  // Strip the reset that truncateToWidth placed immediately before the\n  // ellipsis and any codes it preserved/added after the ellipsis. The suffix\n  // (e.g. a status/runtime marker) starts at the first non-ANSI char after\n  // the trailing reset run.\n  const beforeReset = clipped\n    .slice(0, ellipsisIndex)\n    .replace(/(?:\\x1b\\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z])+$/, \"\");\n  const afterEllipsis = clipped\n    .slice(ellipsisIndex + 1)\n    .replace(/^(?:\\x1b\\[[0-9;]*[A-Za-z])+/, \"\");\n  const prefix = `${beforeReset}…`;\n  const { fgOpens, fgCloses, boldOpens, boldCloses } = styleBalance(prefix);\n  const closes: string[] = [];\n  if (boldOpens > boldCloses) closes.push(\"\\x1b[22m\");\n  if (fgOpens > fgCloses) closes.push(\"\\x1b[39m\");\n  return `${prefix}${closes.join(\"\")}${afterEllipsis}`;\n}\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { visibleWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\nimport type { Tone } from \"../shared/ui-theme.ts\";\nimport { crop } from \"./layout.ts\";\n\n/**\n * The only background fills Aurora ever paints. Pi's public `Theme.bg` accepts\n * exactly these eight tokens, so tiles and pills stay correct in every theme\n * (including `light`) instead of hardcoding ANSI colours. The type is derived\n * from the API itself: `ThemeBg` is not exported by the package entry.\n */\nexport type TileFill = Parameters<Theme[\"bg\"]>[0];\n\nexport interface TileInput {\n  title: string;\n  lines: readonly string[];\n  badge?: string;\n  tone?: \"accent\" | \"muted\" | \"warning\" | \"error\" | \"success\";\n  /** Card background. Omit it and the tile degrades to the flat panel look. */\n  fill?: TileFill;\n}\n\n/** The neutral card surface every tile uses unless a status owns its fill. */\nexport const NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL: TileFill = \"toolPendingBg\";\n\n/**\n * Maps a status tone to the card fill that carries it. Only success and error\n * have dedicated background tokens; everything else sits on the neutral card.\n */\nexport function statusFill(tone: TileInput[\"tone\"]): TileFill {\n  if (tone === \"success\") return \"toolSuccessBg\";\n  if (tone === \"error\") return \"toolErrorBg\";\n  return NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL;\n}\n\n/**\n * A status chip. Loud tones get a filled background (or inverse, since Pi has\n * no warning background token); routine tones stay flat text so a bar of many\n * segments does not turn into a wall of colour.\n */\nexport function renderPill(theme: Theme, text: string, tone: Tone): string {\n  switch (tone) {\n    case \"accent\":\n      return theme.bg(\n        \"selectedBg\",\n        ` ${theme.fg(\"accent\", theme.bold(text))} `,\n      );\n    case \"success\":\n      return theme.bg(\"toolSuccessBg\", ` ${theme.fg(\"success\", text)} `);\n    case \"error\":\n      return theme.bg(\n        \"toolErrorBg\",\n        ` ${theme.fg(\"error\", theme.bold(text))} `,\n      );\n    case \"warning\":\n      return theme.inverse(` ${theme.fg(\"warning\", text)} `);\n    default:\n      return theme.fg(tone, text);\n  }\n}\n\n/** Terminal cells a pill occupies: its text plus one padding cell each side. */\nexport function pillExtraCells(tone: Tone): number {\n  return tone === \"accent\" ||\n    tone === \"success\" ||\n    tone === \"error\" ||\n    tone === \"warning\"\n    ? 2\n    : 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * A labelled field row: muted uppercase label, then the value. Callers style\n * the value themselves; the tile crops the finished row to its inner width.\n */\nexport function renderField(\n  theme: Theme,\n  label: string,\n  value: string,\n): string {\n  return `${theme.fg(\"muted\", theme.bold(label.toUpperCase()))}  ${value}`;\n}\n\nfunction padFilled(\n  theme: Theme,\n  value: string,\n  width: number,\n  fill: TileFill,\n): string {\n  // crop closes any fg/bold left open by the truncation, so the tile fill is\n  // not punctured by a stray full reset and no colour bleeds past this row\n  // into the frame or a neighbouring tile.\n  const clipped = crop(value, width);\n  const padding = \" \".repeat(Math.max(0, width - visibleWidth(clipped)));\n  return `${theme.bg(fill, clipped)}${theme.bg(fill, padding)}`;\n}\n\n/** One empty, filled card row — used to equalise tile heights in a grid. */\nexport function tileBlankRow(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  fill?: TileFill,\n): string {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  if (!fill || available < 18) return \"\";\n  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, available - 4);\n  const border = (value: string) => theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", value);\n  return `${border(\"│ \")}${padFilled(theme, \"\", innerWidth, fill)}${border(\" │\")}`;\n}\n\n/** Card rows: two frame rows plus one row per content line. */\nexport function tileHeight(input: TileInput): number {\n  return input.lines.length + 2;\n}\n\n/**\n * A filled card: the frame shell, but the title row and every content row are\n * padded to the full inner width and painted with `theme.bg`, so the card\n * reads as one solid surface. The < 18 column fallback drops the frame\n * entirely and preserves the content, as the old panel shell did.\n */\nexport function renderTile(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  input: TileInput,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const title = theme.fg(input.tone ?? \"accent\", theme.bold(input.title));\n  const badge = input.badge\n    ? ` ${renderPill(theme, input.badge, input.tone ?? \"muted\")}`\n    : \"\";\n\n  if (available < 18) {\n    return [\n      crop(`${title}${badge}`, available),\n      ...input.lines.map((line) => crop(line, available)),\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, available - 4);\n  const border = (value: string) => theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", value);\n\n  if (!input.fill) {\n    // Unfilled tiles keep the classic framed look without a background.\n    const heading = crop(`${title}${badge}`, innerWidth);\n    const fillDashes = \"─\".repeat(\n      Math.max(0, innerWidth - visibleWidth(heading)),\n    );\n    const row = (value: string) => {\n      const clipped = crop(value, innerWidth);\n      return `${border(\"│ \")}${clipped}${\" \".repeat(\n        Math.max(0, innerWidth - visibleWidth(clipped)),\n      )}${border(\" │\")}`;\n    };\n    return [\n      `${border(\"╭─\")}${heading}${border(`${fillDashes}─╮`)}`,\n      ...input.lines.map(row),\n      border(`╰${\"─\".repeat(Math.max(1, available - 2))}╯`),\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const fill = input.fill;\n  const heading = padFilled(theme, `${title}${badge}`, innerWidth, fill);\n  const row = (value: string) =>\n    `${border(\"│ \")}${padFilled(theme, value, innerWidth, fill)}${border(\" │\")}`;\n\n  return [\n    `${border(\"╭─\")}${heading}${border(\"─╮\")}`,\n    ...input.lines.map(row),\n    border(`╰${\"─\".repeat(Math.max(1, available - 2))}╯`),\n  ];\n}\n\n/**\n * Lays tiles out as a grid. With `columns: 2` consecutive tiles share a row:\n * both get half the width, the shorter one is padded with blank filled rows.\n * An unpaired last tile spans the full width. Widths under two framed tiles\n * degrade to a vertical stack, and a single column always stacks.\n */\nexport function renderTileGrid(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  tiles: readonly TileInput[],\n  columns: 1 | 2 = 1,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  // Two framed tiles side by side need 2 × 18 columns plus one gap column.\n  const framedPairFits = available >= 37;\n  if (columns !== 2 || !framedPairFits) {\n    return tiles.flatMap((tile) => renderTile(theme, available, tile));\n  }\n\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  const columnWidth = Math.floor((available - 1) / 2);\n  for (let index = 0; index < tiles.length; index += 2) {\n    const left = tiles[index]!;\n    const right = tiles[index + 1];\n    if (!right) {\n      lines.push(...renderTile(theme, available, left));\n      continue;\n    }\n    const leftLines = renderTile(theme, columnWidth, left);\n    const rightLines = renderTile(theme, columnWidth, right);\n    const height = Math.max(leftLines.length, rightLines.length);\n    for (let row = 0; row < height; row += 1) {\n      const leftLine =\n        leftLines[row] ?? tileBlankRow(theme, columnWidth, left.fill);\n      const rightLine =\n        rightLines[row] ?? tileBlankRow(theme, columnWidth, right.fill);\n      lines.push(`${leftLine} ${rightLine}`);\n    }\n  }\n  return lines;\n}\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { visibleWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\nimport { footerTier, type Layout } from \"../shared/layout.ts\";\nimport {\n  renderSegment,\n  STATUS_SEPARATOR,\n  statusSeparator,\n  type Tone,\n} from \"../shared/ui-theme.ts\";\nimport { compactCwd } from \"./cwd.ts\";\nimport { crop } from \"./layout.ts\";\nimport type { AuroraUiState } from \"./state.ts\";\nimport { pillExtraCells, renderPill } from \"./tile.ts\";\nimport { thinkingLabel, thinkingTone } from \"./thinking.ts\";\n\n/**\n * The footer is the one permanent status surface, and it is one line.\n *\n * Everything it reports comes out of runtime state that some other extension\n * already publishes on the Aurora bus or through `getExtensionStatuses()`.\n * Nothing here starts a process, probes git or the LSP, asks a provider or\n * touches the file system: `render` is called on every frame, so any of that\n * would be paid for continuously and for nothing.\n */\nexport interface FooterInput {\n  state: AuroraUiState;\n  statuses: ReadonlyMap<string, string>;\n  contextPercent: number | null;\n  /** Captured from ExtensionContext at session start; never read from render I/O. */\n  cwd?: string;\n  homeDirectory?: string;\n  /** True while a dashboard surface (auto/compact/expanded) is active: it owns\n   * routine verification reporting, so the footer stops duplicating a plain\n   * \"verified\". Failed/stale checks stay critical footer risks regardless. */\n  dashboardVisible?: boolean;\n}\n\nconst CONTEXT_WARNING_PERCENT = 70;\nconst CONTEXT_CRITICAL_PERCENT = 90;\nconst MODEL_MAX_COLUMNS = 32;\nconst FOLDER_MAX_COLUMNS: Record<Layout, number> = {\n  wide: 34,\n  comfortable: 24,\n  standard: 14,\n  compact: 10,\n};\n\n/**\n * Where a segment sits on the line. Reading order is fixed and independent of\n * how hard the segment fights to stay: risk is loud, but it belongs at the end\n * where a scan finishes, not wedged between the model and the thinking level.\n * A plain object rather than a `const enum` — the test loader transpiles\n * without type information.\n */\nconst Slot = {\n  workflow: 0,\n  model: 1,\n  thinking: 2,\n  folder: 3,\n  context: 4,\n  verification: 5,\n  risk: 6,\n} as const;\n\ntype Slot = (typeof Slot)[keyof typeof Slot];\n\n/**\n * What gets dropped first when the line is too long — lowest survives longest.\n * The workflow is last man standing. Everything that reports an actual problem\n * outranks the model name: at the widths where this matters the user knows\n * which model they picked, and does not yet know their language server died.\n */\nconst Priority = {\n  workflow: 0,\n  yolo: 1,\n  recovery: 2,\n  failedVerification: 3,\n  exhaustedContext: 4,\n  lsp: 5,\n  folder: 6,\n  model: 7,\n  verification: 8,\n  thinking: 9,\n  context: 10,\n} as const;\n\ntype Priority = (typeof Priority)[keyof typeof Priority];\n\ninterface Segment {\n  slot: Slot;\n  priority: Priority;\n  text: string;\n  tone: Tone;\n  bold?: boolean;\n  /**\n   * A segment that reports a risk or a failure. It ignores the width tier,\n   * because a footer that hides YOLO or a failed verification to save four\n   * columns is worse than one that has to drop something else.\n   */\n  critical?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** Which routine segments each width tier may show at all. */\nconst ROUTINE_TIERS: Record<Layout, ReadonlySet<Slot>> = {\n  wide: new Set([\n    Slot.workflow,\n    Slot.model,\n    Slot.thinking,\n    Slot.folder,\n    Slot.context,\n    Slot.verification,\n  ]),\n  comfortable: new Set([\n    Slot.workflow,\n    Slot.model,\n    Slot.thinking,\n    Slot.folder,\n    Slot.context,\n    Slot.verification,\n  ]),\n  standard: new Set([Slot.workflow, Slot.model, Slot.thinking, Slot.folder]),\n  compact: new Set([Slot.workflow, Slot.model, Slot.folder]),\n};\n\nfunction verificationState(input: FooterInput): string | null {\n  const value = input.statuses.get(\"verification\");\n  return value ? value.replace(/^Verify:\\s*/, \"\") : null;\n}\n\nfunction verificationTone(state: string): Tone {\n  if (state === \"verified\") return \"success\";\n  if (state === \"unchanged\") return \"muted\";\n  if (state === \"checks_failed\") return \"error\";\n  return \"warning\";\n}\n\n/** Only an unproven or failing workspace is worth defending space for. */\nfunction verificationNeedsAttention(state: string | null): boolean {\n  return state !== null && state !== \"unchanged\" && state !== \"verified\";\n}\n\nfunction lspState(input: FooterInput): string {\n  return input.statuses.get(\"lsp\") ?? input.state.lsp.state ?? \"—\";\n}\n\n/**\n * A working, idle or disabled language server is not news, so it stays off the\n * line entirely. Only a degraded one is, and it claims space at any width.\n *\n * `extensions/lsp/status.ts` publishes a closed set: `aus`, `leerlauf`,\n * `eingeschränkt` and `${n} aktiv`. Matching the one bad value is what keeps\n * `3 aktiv` — a perfectly healthy server count — out of the warning slot.\n */\nfunction lspNeedsAttention(state: string): boolean {\n  return state === \"eingeschränkt\";\n}\n\nfunction collectSegments(input: FooterInput, width: number): Segment[] {\n  const tier = footerTier(width);\n  const segments: Segment[] = [\n    {\n      slot: Slot.workflow,\n      priority: Priority.workflow,\n      text: input.state.workflow.label,\n      tone: \"accent\",\n      bold: true,\n    },\n    {\n      slot: Slot.model,\n      priority: Priority.model,\n      // The real runtime model id. Prettifying it into a marketing name would\n      // mean inventing a mapping the runtime never gave us.\n      text: crop(input.state.model.id ?? \"kein Modell\", MODEL_MAX_COLUMNS),\n      tone: \"text\",\n    },\n  ];\n\n  if (input.state.model.thinking) {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.thinking,\n      priority: Priority.thinking,\n      text: `Denken ${thinkingLabel(input.state.model.thinking)}`,\n      tone: thinkingTone(input.state.model.thinking),\n      bold: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (input.cwd) {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.folder,\n      priority: Priority.folder,\n      text: compactCwd(\n        input.cwd,\n        FOLDER_MAX_COLUMNS[tier],\n        input.homeDirectory,\n      ),\n      tone: \"muted\",\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (input.contextPercent !== null) {\n    // Colour and layout priority are separate judgements. A filling context is\n    // worth a warning colour long before it is worth taking a narrow line's\n    // space away from the workflow — only a nearly exhausted one is urgent\n    // enough to outrank the tier it would normally be filtered out by.\n    const exhausted = input.contextPercent >= CONTEXT_CRITICAL_PERCENT;\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.context,\n      priority: exhausted ? Priority.exhaustedContext : Priority.context,\n      text: `Kontext ${Math.round(input.contextPercent)}%`,\n      tone: exhausted\n        ? \"error\"\n        : input.contextPercent >= CONTEXT_WARNING_PERCENT\n          ? \"warning\"\n          : \"muted\",\n      critical: exhausted,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const verification = verificationState(input);\n  if (verification !== null) {\n    const attention = verificationNeedsAttention(verification);\n    // Ownership rule: a successful check belongs to the visible dashboard\n    // (or the inspector when auto mode shows nothing right now); the footer\n    // only carries verification that demands attention.\n    const ownedByDashboard = input.dashboardVisible === true && !attention;\n    if (!ownedByDashboard) {\n      segments.push({\n        slot: Slot.verification,\n        priority: attention ? Priority.failedVerification : Priority.verification,\n        text: `${verification === \"verified\" ? \"✓\" : attention ? \"⚠\" : \"\"} ${verification}`.trim(),\n        tone: verificationTone(verification),\n        // Without a dashboard surface owning the verdict, the routine success\n        // stays visible in the footer — but it is not a risk: it keeps normal\n        // priority and yields to real risks on narrow lines like any metadata.\n        critical: attention,\n      });\n    }\n  }\n\n  const lsp = lspState(input);\n  if (lspNeedsAttention(lsp)) {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.risk,\n      priority: Priority.lsp,\n      text: `⚠ LSP ${lsp}`,\n      tone: \"error\",\n      critical: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  if (input.state.permissions.level === \"yolo\") {\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.risk,\n      priority: Priority.yolo,\n      text: \"⚠ YOLO\",\n      tone: \"error\",\n      bold: true,\n      critical: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  const recovery = input.statuses.get(\"recovery\");\n  if (recovery) {\n    // Eine offene Recovery-Sperre ist eine aktive Schreibgrenze und bleibt\n    // deshalb unabhängig von der Breite sichtbar.\n    segments.push({\n      slot: Slot.risk,\n      priority: Priority.recovery,\n      text: recovery,\n      tone: \"error\",\n      bold: true,\n      critical: true,\n    });\n  }\n\n  return segments;\n}\n\n/**\n * Terminal cells, not JavaScript characters. `⚠`, `✓`, CJK and emoji all cost\n * more cells than they do string length, and a selection that measured length\n * would believe a line fits and then have to truncate a finished segment at\n * the edge — the one thing this layout exists to avoid. Pills pay their two\n * padding cells here too, so the width budget sees exactly what renders.\n */\nfunction widthOf(segments: readonly Segment[], pillTier: boolean): number {\n  if (segments.length === 0) return 0;\n  return (\n    segments.reduce(\n      (total, segment) =>\n        total + visibleWidth(segment.text) + segmentCells(segment, pillTier),\n      0,\n    ) + visibleWidth(STATUS_SEPARATOR) * (segments.length - 1)\n  );\n}\n\n/** Whether the footer renders segments as filled pills at this tier. */\nfunction isPillTier(tier: Layout): boolean {\n  return tier === \"comfortable\" || tier === \"wide\";\n}\n\n/**\n * Whether this segment renders as a pill when the tier allows it: workflow\n * identity and every critical risk; routine metadata never does. Shared by\n * the width budget (`segmentCells`) and the actual render decision below, so\n * the two can never silently drift apart.\n */\nfunction isPillSegment(segment: Segment): boolean {\n  return segment.critical || segment.slot === Slot.workflow;\n}\n\n/** The extra cells a segment pays when it renders as a filled pill. */\nfunction segmentCells(segment: Segment, pillTier: boolean): number {\n  if (!pillTier || !isPillSegment(segment)) return 0;\n  return pillExtraCells(segment.tone);\n}\n\n/**\n * Keep what the width tier allows plus everything critical, then, while the\n * result is still too wide, drop the least important segment that is left.\n * Segments give up their place entirely rather than being shaved at the edge,\n * so whatever remains stays readable — and the survivors are then put back\n * into reading order, which is not the order they were dropped in.\n */\nfunction selectFooterSegments(\n  segments: readonly Segment[],\n  width: number,\n): Segment[] {\n  const tier = footerTier(width);\n  const pillTier = isPillTier(tier);\n  const allowed = ROUTINE_TIERS[tier];\n  const kept = segments.filter(\n    (segment) => segment.critical || allowed.has(segment.slot),\n  );\n  while (kept.length > 1 && widthOf(kept, pillTier) > width) {\n    let victim = 0;\n    for (let index = 1; index < kept.length; index += 1)\n      if (kept[index]!.priority > kept[victim]!.priority) victim = index;\n    kept.splice(victim, 1);\n  }\n  return kept.sort((a, b) => a.slot - b.slot);\n}\n\nexport function renderFooterLines(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  input: FooterInput,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const selected = selectFooterSegments(\n    collectSegments(input, available),\n    available,\n  );\n  // From comfortable width on, the workflow identity and every risk read as\n  // filled chips; routine metadata stays flat so the line never turns into a\n  // wall of colour. Narrow tiers keep the flat look entirely.\n  const pillTier = isPillTier(footerTier(available));\n  const line = selected\n    .map((segment) =>\n      pillTier && isPillSegment(segment)\n        ? renderPill(theme, segment.text, segment.tone)\n        : renderSegment(theme, segment.text, {\n            tone: segment.tone,\n            bold: segment.bold,\n          }),\n    )\n    .join(statusSeparator(theme));\n  return [visibleWidth(line) > available ? crop(line, available) : line];\n}\n","exit_code":0,"status":"completed"}}

Only real failures or explicit\n * attention events ever get warning/error tone.\n */\nconst QUIET_STILL_THRESHOLD_MS = 15_000;\nconst QUIET_INFO_THRESHOLD_MS = 30_000;\n\n/** Presentation-only categories; tool execution remains entirely in Pi core. */\nexport type ActivityToolKind =\n  | \"read\"\n  | \"search\"\n  | \"edit\"\n  | \"bash\"\n  | \"lsp\"\n  | \"test\"\n  | \"verification\"\n  | \"subagent\"\n  | \"wait\"\n  | \"web\"\n  | \"generic\";\n\nexport type ActivityToolTone = \"running\" | \"warning\" | \"error\";\n\nexport interface ActiveToolView {\n  id: string;\n  name: string;\n  kind?: ActivityToolKind;\n  target?: string;\n  startedAt: number;\n  /** Timestamp of the last observed progress (e.g. new bash output). */\n  lastUpdateAt?: number;\n  tone?: ActivityToolTone;\n}\n\n/**\n * A subagent the current turn started. The command is never executed or parsed by\n * a shell; this only chooses a presentation label for the real bash event.\n */\nfunction commandStarts(command: string, expression: string): boolean {\n  return new RegExp(`(?:^|(?:&&|\\\\|\\\\||;|\\\\n)\\\\s*)${expression}`, \"i\").test(\n    command,\n  );\n}\n\nfunction isVerificationCommand(command: string): boolean {\n  const packageManager = \"(?:npm|pnpm|yarn|bun)\";\n  return (\n    commandStarts(\n      command,\n      `${packageManager}(?:\\\\s+--[^\\\\s]+(?:\\\\s+[^\\\\s]+)?)?\\\\s+run\\\\s+verify(?:\\\\s|$)`,\n    ) || commandStarts(command, `${packageManager}\\\\s+verify(?:\\\\s|$)`)\n  );\n}\n\nfunction isTestCommand(command: string): boolean {\n  const packageManager = \"(?:npm|pnpm|yarn|bun)\";\n  return (\n    commandStarts(\n      command,\n      `${packageManager}(?:\\\\s+--[^\\\\s]+(?:\\\\s+[^\\\\s]+)?)?\\\\s+(?:run\\\\s+)?test(?:\\\\s|$)`,\n    ) ||\n    commandStarts(\n      command,\n      \"(?:npx|pnpx|yarn\\\\s+dlx|bunx\\\\s+)?(?:vitest|jest|pytest)(?:\\\\s|$)\",\n    ) ||\n    commandStarts(command, \"cargo\\\\s+test(?:\\\\s|$)\") ||\n    commandStarts(command, \"go\\\\s+test(?:\\\\s|$)\")\n  );\n}\n\n/** Classify only names and arguments that the running Pi runtime actually emits. */\nexport function classifyTool(\n  toolName: string,\n  args: unknown,\n): ActivityToolKind {\n  const name = toolName.toLowerCase();\n  switch (name) {\n    case \"read\":\n      return \"read\";\n    case \"grep\":\n    case \"find\":\n      return \"search\";\n    case \"edit\":\n    case \"write\":\n      return \"edit\";\n    case \"verify\":\n    case \"project_check\":\n      return \"verification\";\n    case \"subagent\":\n      return \"subagent\";\n    case \"bash\";\n      if (isVerificationCommand(command)) return \"verification\";\n      return isTestCommand(command) ? \"test\" : \"bash\";\n    }\n    default:\n      return LSP_TOOLS.has(name) ? \"lsp\" : \"generic\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction pathWithPosition(cwd: string, args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const path = firstString(args, [\"path\"]);\n  if (!path) return undefined;\n  const relPath = toWorkspaceRelative(cwd, path);\n  const line = numberValue(args, \"line\");\n  const character = numberValue(args, \"character\");\n  if (line === undefined || character === undefined) return relPath;\n  return `${relPath}:${line + 1}:${character + 1}`;\n}\n\nfunction lspTarget(\n  cwd: string,\n  toolName: string,\n  args: unknown,\n): string | undefined {\n  switch (toolName.toLowerCase()) {\n    case \"lsp_diagnostics\": {\n      const path = firstString(args, [\"path\"]);\n      return `Diagnosen · ${path ? toWorkspaceRelative(cwd, path) : \"\"}`.trim();\n    }\n    case \"lsp_definition\":\n      return `Definition · ${pathWithPosition(cwd, args) ?? \"\"}`.trim();\n    case \"lsp_references\":\n      return `Referenzen · ${pathWithPosition(cwd, args) ?? \"\"}`.trim();\n    case \"lsp_hover\":\n      return `Info · ${pathWithPosition(cwd, args) ?? \"\"}`.trim();\n    case \"lsp_workspace_symbols\": {\n      const query = firstString(args, [\"query\"]);\n      return query\n        ? `Workspace-Symbole · ${quote(query)}`\n        : \"Workspace-Symbole\";\n    }\n    default:\n      return undefined;\n  }\n}\n\nfunction agentNames(value: unknown): string[] {\n  if (!Array.isArray(value)) return [];\n  return value.flatMap((item) => {\n    const entry = record(item);\n    if (!entry) return [];\n    const agent = typeof entry.agent === \"string\" ? entry.agent : undefined;\n    return [\n      ...(agent ? [agent] : []),\n      ...agentNames(entry.tasks),\n      ...agentNames(entry.parallel),\n    ];\n  });\n}\n\nfunction subagentTarget(args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const input = record(args);\n  if (!input) return undefined;\n  if (typeof input.action === \"string\") return input.action;\n  if (typeof input.agent === \"string\") return input.agent;\n  const agents = [...agentNames(input.tasks), ...agentNames(input.chain)];\n  const unique = [...new Set(agents)];\n  if (unique.length === 1) return unique[0];\n  if (unique.length > 1) return `${unique.length} agents`;\n  if (Array.isArray(input.tasks)) return `parallel · ${input.tasks.length}`;\n  if (Array.isArray(input.chain)) return `chain · ${input.chain.length}`;\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction verificationTarget(args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const input = record(args);\n  if (!input) return undefined;\n  if (typeof input.check === \"string\") return input.check;\n  if (typeof input.profile === \"string\") return input.profile;\n  if (\n    Array.isArray(input.profiles) &&\n    input.profiles.every((profile) => typeof profile === \"string\")\n  )\n    return input.profiles.join(\", \");\n  return undefined;\n}\n\nfunction searchTarget(\n  cwd: string,\n  toolName: string,\n  args: unknown,\n): string | undefined {\n  const query = firstString(args, [\"query\", \"pattern\"]);\n  if (query) return quote(query);\n  if (toolName.toLowerCase() !== \"find\") return undefined;\n  const path = firstString(args, [\"path\"]);\n  return path ? toWorkspaceRelative(cwd, path) : undefined;\n}\n\nfunction genericTarget(cwd: string, args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const path = firstString(args, [\"path\", \"file_path\", \"file\"]);\n  if (path) return toWorkspaceRelative(cwd, path);\n  return firstString(args, [\n    \"query\",\n    \"pattern\",\n    \"url\",\n    \"symbol\",\n    \"name\",\n    \"target\",\n  ]);\n}\n\nfunction waitTarget(args: unknown): string | undefined {\n  const input = record(args);\n  if (!input) return undefined;\n  if (input.all === true) return \"alle Subagenten\";\n  return firstString(args, [\"agent\", \"runId\", \"target\"]);\n}\n\n/** Derive an Activity line entirely from the tool execution start payload. */\nexport function describeToolActivity(\n  toolName: string,\n  args: unknown,\n  cwd: string,\n): Pick<ActiveToolView, \"kind\" | \"target\"> {\n  const kind = classifyTool(toolName, args);\n  switch (kind) {\n    case \"read\":\n    case \"edit\": {\n      const path = firstString(args, [\"path\"]);\n      return {\n        kind,\n        target: path ? toWorkspaceRelative(cwd, path) : undefined,\n      };\n    }\n    case \"search\":\n      return { kind, target: searchTarget(cwd, toolName, args) };\n    case \"bash\":\n    case \"test\":\n      return { kind, target: normalizedCommand(args) };\n    case \"lsp\":\n      return { kind, target: lspTarget(cwd, toolName, args) };\n    case \"verification\":\n      return {\n        kind,\n        target:\n          toolName.toLowerCase() === \"bash\"\n            ? normalizedCommand(args)\n            : verificationTarget(args),\n      };\n    case \"subagent\":\n      return { kind, target: subagentTarget(args) };\n    case \"wait\":\n      return { kind, target: waitTarget(args) };\n    case \"web\":\n    case \"generic\":\n      return { kind, target: genericTarget(cwd, args) };\n  }\n}\n\nexport function toolPresentation(\n  name: string,\n  args?: unknown,\n): ToolPresentation {\n  const kind = classifyTool(name, args);\n  const presentation = PRESENTATIONS[kind];\n  return kind === \"generic\" && name.trim()\n    ? { ...presentation, label: `${presentation.label} · ${name}` }\n    : presentation;\n}\n\nfunction toolStatus(\n  tool: ActiveToolView,\n  now: number,\n): {\n  tone: \"accent\" | \"warning\" | \"error\" | \"muted\";\n  label: string;\n  label: string;\n} {\n  if (tool.tone === \"error\") return { tone: \"error\", label: \"FEHLER\" };\n  if (tool.tone === \"warning\") return { tone: \"warning\", label: \"HINWEIS\" };\n  const sinceUpdate = now - (tool.lastUpdateAt ?? tool.startedAt);\n  // Neutral silence is not an alarm: first a calm still-active marker,\n  // then the silent duration itself — both without warning tone.\n  // Evidence-backed problems carry their own tone via tool.tone above.\n  if (sinceUpdate >= QUIET_INFO_THRESHOLD_MS) {\n    return {\n      tone: \"muted\",\n      label: `${Math.floor(sinceUpdate / 1000)}s ohne neue Ausgabe`,\n    };\n  }\n  if (sinceUpdate >= QUIET_STILL_THRESHOLD_MS) {\n    return { tone: \"muted\", label: \"STILL AKTIV\" };\n  }\n  return { tone: \"accent\", label: \"LÄUFT\" };\n}\n\n/**\n * The one status tally every overflow line uses. Counting in more than one\n * place is how the same state ends up named two different ways in one frame.\n */\nfunction tally(labels: readonly string[]): string {\n  const counts = new Map<string, number>();\n  for (const label of labels) counts.set(label, (counts.get(label) ?? 0) + 1);\n  return [...counts.entries()]\n    .map(([label, count]) => `${count} ${label}`)\n    .join(\", \");\n}\n\nfunction toolTally(tools: readonly ActiveToolView[], now: number): string {\n  return tally(tools.map((tool) => toolStatus(tool, now).label.toLowerCase()));\n}\n\nfunction subagentTally(subagents: readonly SubagentInfo[]): string {\n  return tally(\n    subagents.map((entry) => subagentStatusLabel(entry.status).toLowerCase()),\n  );\n}\n\nfunction toolSummary(tools: readonly ActiveToolView[], now: number): string {\n  const statuses = toolTally(tools, now);\n  return `+${tools.length} weitere Tools${statuses ? ` · ${statuses}` : \"\"}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * The activity widget's combined overflow line. It lives here, next to the row\n * renderers, so the labels and the tally have exactly one source; the widget\n * only decides how many rows fit.\n */\nexport function hiddenActivitySummary(\n  hiddenTools: readonly ActiveToolView[],\n  hiddenSubagents: readonly SubagentInfo[],\n  now: number,\n): string {\n  const parts: string[] = [];\n  if (hiddenTools.length > 0) {\n    const statuses = toolTally(hiddenTools, now);\n    parts.push(\n      `${hiddenTools.length} Tools${statuses ? ` · ${statuses}` : \"\"}`,\n    );\n  }\n  if (hiddenSubagents.length > 0) {\n    const statuses = subagentTally(hiddenSubagents);\n    parts.push(\n      `${hiddenSubagents.length} Subagenten${statuses ? ` · ${statuses}` : \"\"}`,\n    );\n  }\n  return `↳ +${parts.join(\" · \")}`;\n}\n\nfunction padToWidth(value: string, width: number): string {\n  return `${value}${\" \".repeat(Math.max(0, width - visibleWidth(value)))}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Renders lifecycle metadata only. Aurora deliberately does not re-register or\n * wrap tools, so argument validation, execution, cancellation, updates and\n * results continue to be handled exactly by Pi's core tool definitions.\n */\nexport function renderActiveTools(\n  tools: readonly ActiveToolView[],\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  now: number,\n  options: {\n    compact?: boolean;\n    wide?: boolean;\n    limit?: number;\n    /** Omit the per-row LÄUFT/elapsed suffix when the activity heading above\n     * already says exactly that — one fact, one row. */\n    suppressRunningStatus?: boolean;\n  } = {},\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const compact = options.compact ?? footerTier(width) === \"compact\";\n  const wide = options.wide ?? footerTier(width) === \"wide\";\n  const limit = options.limit ?? (compact ? 1 : 3);\n  const visible = tools.slice(0, limit).map((tool) => {\n    const elapsed = Math.max(0, Math.floor((now - tool.startedAt) / 1000));\n    const presentation =\n      tool.kind === undefined || tool.kind === \"generic\"\n        ? toolPresentation(tool.name)\n        : PRESENTATIONS[tool.kind];\n    // A checkmark would falsely claim success while the verification is still\n    // running. Completed tools disappear from this transient surface, so only\n    // Pi's real result renderer may show the success glyph.\n    const glyph =\n      tool.kind === \"verification\" ? RUNNING_GLYPH : presentation.glyph;\n    const status = toolStatus(tool, now);\n    const marker = theme.fg(status.tone, glyph);\n    const label = compact\n      ? theme.bold(presentation.label)\n      : padToWidth(theme.bold(presentation.label), 12);\n    const statusIsPlainRunning =\n      status.tone === \"accent\" && status.label === \"LÄUFT\";\n    const suffix =\n      options.suppressRunningStatus && statusIsPlainRunning\n        ? \"\"\n        : ` · ${theme.fg(status.tone, status.label)} · ${theme.fg(\"dim\", `${elapsed}s`)}`;\n    if (!tool.target) {\n      return crop(`${marker} ${label}${suffix}`, available);\n    }\n    // The target field is ellipsized on its own, leaf-preserving, so a long\n    // path never eats into the status/runtime suffix — the one part a\n    // failing tool most needs visible.\n    const targetWidth = Math.max(\n      1,\n      available - visibleWidth(`${marker} ${label} `) - visibleWidth(suffix),\n    );\n    const shortened = ellipsizeMiddle(tool.target, targetWidth);\n    const target = theme.fg(\n      \"muted\",\n      wide ? padToWidth(shortened, targetWidth) : shortened,\n    );\n    return `${marker} ${label} ${target}${suffix}`;\n  });\n  const hidden = tools.slice(visible.length);\n  if (hidden.length > 0)\n    visible.push(\n      crop(\n        theme.fg(\"muted\", `↳ ${toolSummary(hidden, now)}`),\n        available,\n      ),\n    );\n  return visible;\n}\n\nfunction subagentTone(\n  status: SubagentInfo[\"status\"],\n): \"accent\" | \"warning\" | \"error\" | \"muted\" {\n  switch (status) {\n    // Matches the \"running\" tone of a normal tool row — green stays reserved\n    // for real, finished success (see themes/aurora-night.json `success`).\n    case \"running\":\n      return \"accent\";\n    case \"paused\":\n      return \"warning\";\n    case \"needs_attention\":\n      return \"error\";\n    case \"queued\":\n      return \"muted\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction subagentGlyph(status: SubagentInfo[\"status\"]): string {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"running\":\n      return \"◉\";\n    case \"queued\":\n      return \"○\";\n    case \"paused\":\n      return \"◌\";\n    case \"needs_attention\":\n      return \"!\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction subagentStatusLabel(status: SubagentInfo[\"status\"]): string {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"running\":\n      return \"LÄUFT\";\n    case \"queued\":\n      return \"IM HINTERGRUND\";\n    case \"paused\":\n      return \"PAUSIERT\";\n    case \"needs_attention\":\n      return \"AUFMERKSAMKEIT\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction subagentSummary(subagents: readonly SubagentInfo[]): string {\n  return `+${subagents.length} weitere · ${subagentTally(subagents)}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * Subagents appear inline with the work that started them, for as long as that\n * work runs — no permanent tab strip, no navigation level of their own. When\n * the turn settles the widget goes away with everything else in it.\n */\nexport function renderSubagents(\n  subagents: readonly SubagentInfo[],\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  options: { compact?: boolean; limit?: number; summary?: boolean } = {},\n): string[] {\n  if (subagents.length === 0) return [];\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const compact = options.compact ?? footerTier(width) === \"compact\";\n  const clip = (value: string) => crop(value, available);\n  const attention = subagents.filter(\n    (entry) => entry.status === \"needs_attention\",\n  ).length;\n  const summary = clip(\n    compact\n      ? `${theme.fg(\"accent\", \"◉\")} ${theme.bold(\"SUBAGENTS\")} ${theme.fg(\"muted\", `· ${subagents.length}`)}`\n      : theme.bold(theme.fg(\"border\", `SUBAGENTS · ${subagents.length}`)),\n  );\n  const attentionLine = attention\n    ? theme.fg(\"error\", ` · ${attention} Aufmerksamkeit`)\n    : \"\";\n  const summaryWithAttention = clip(`${summary}${attentionLine}`);\n  // Whatever needs a decision is worth the few lines this surface has.\n  const ordered = [...subagents].sort(\n    (a, b) =>\n      Number(b.status === \"needs_attention\") -\n      Number(a.status === \"needs_attention\"),\n  );\n  const limit = options.limit ?? (compact ? 0 : 3);\n  const visible = ordered.slice(0, limit);\n  const lines = visible.map((entry) => {\n    const glyph = theme.fg(\n      subagentTone(entry.status),\n      subagentGlyph(entry.status),\n    );\n    const detail = entry.phase ?? entry.label;\n    const suffix = detail ? ` · ${theme.fg(\"muted\", detail)}` : \"\";\n    return clip(\n      `   ${glyph} ${theme.bold(entry.agent)} · ${theme.fg(subagentTone(entry.status), subagentStatusLabel(entry.status))}${suffix}`,\n    );\n  });\n  const hidden = ordered.slice(visible.length);\n  if (hidden.length > 0)\n    lines.push(clip(`   ${theme.fg(\"muted\", `↳ ${subagentSummary(hidden)}`)}`));\n  return options.summary === false ? lines : [summaryWithAttention, ...lines];\n}\n\nconst PHASES: Array<{ id: TaskPhase; label: string }> = [\n  { id: \"understand\", label: \"Verstehen\" },\n  { id: \"plan\", label: \"Planen\" },\n  { id: \"work\", label: \"Arbeiten\" },\n  { id: \"verify\", label: \"Prüfen\" },\n  { id: \"done\", label: \"Fertig\" },\n];\n\nexport function renderProgressBar(\n  currentPhase: TaskPhase,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n): string {\n  const currentIndex = PHASES.findIndex((p) => p.id === currentPhase);\n  const segments = PHASES.map((phase, index) => {\n    if (index < currentIndex) {\n      return `${theme.fg(\"success\", \"●\")} ${theme.fg(\"muted\", phase.label)}`;\n    }\n    if (index === currentIndex) {\n      return `${theme.fg(\"accent\", \"●\")} ${theme.bold(phase.label)}`;\n    }\n    return `${theme.fg(\"muted\", \"○\")} ${theme.fg(\"dim\", phase.label)}`;\n  });\n  const separator = theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", \" ─ \");\n  return crop(segments.join(separator), width);\n}\n\nfunction branchTone(\n  status: SubagentBranchInfo[\"status\"],\n): \"success\" | \"error\" | \"accent\" | \"warning\" | \"muted\" {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"completed\":\n      return \"success\";\n    case \"failed\":\n    case \"needs_attention\":\n      return \"error\";\n    case \"paused\":\n      return \"warning\";\n    case \"queued\":\n      return \"muted\";\n    case \"running\":\n      return \"accent\";\n  }\n}\n\nfunction branchGlyph(status: SubagentBranchInfo[\"status\"]): string {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"completed\":\n      return \"✓\";\n    case \"failed\":\n      return \"✗\";\n    case \"needs_attention\":\n      return \"!\";\n    case \"paused\":\n      return \"◌\";\n    case \"queued\":\n      return \"○\";\n    case \"running\":\n      return \"●\";\n  }\n}\n\n// Same German vocabulary as subagentStatusLabel() above, so a subagent's\n// status reads the same whether it shows up in the flat SUBAGENTS list or\n// here in the branch tree.\nfunction branchStatusLabel(status: SubagentBranchInfo[\"status\"]): string {\n  switch (status) {\n    case \"completed\":\n      return \"FERTIG\";\n    case \"failed\":\n      return \"FEHLGESCHLAGEN\";\n    case \"needs_attention\":\n      return \"AUFMERKSAMKEIT\";\n    case \"paused\":\n      return \"PAUSIERT\";\n    case \"queued\":\n      return \"IM HINTERGRUND\";\n    case \"running\":\n      return \"LÄUFT\";\n  }\n}\n\nexport function renderSubagentBranches(\n  branches: readonly SubagentBranchInfo[],\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  limit = 3,\n): string[] {\n  if (branches.length === 0) return [];\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  const visible = branches.slice(0, limit);\n\n  for (let i = 0; i < visible.length; i++) {\n    const branch = visible[i]!;\n    const isLast = i === visible.length - 1;\n    const rail = isLast ? \"└─ \" : \"├─ \";\n    const tone = branchTone(branch.status);\n    const glyph = theme.fg(tone, branchGlyph(branch.status));\n    const label = theme.fg(tone, branchStatusLabel(branch.status));\n\n    const header = `${theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", rail)}${theme.bold(branch.agent)} ${glyph} ${label}`;\n    lines.push(crop(header, width));\n\n    if (branch.focus || branch.progress) {\n      const detail = branch.focus ?? branch.progress ?? \"\";\n      const subRail = isLast ? \"   \" : \"│  \";\n      lines.push(\n        crop(\n          `${theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", subRail)}${theme.fg(\"muted\", detail)}`,\n          width,\n        ),\n      );\n    }\n  }\n\n  return lines;\n}\n\nexport function renderVerificationBlock(\n  verification: VerificationViewModel,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n): string[] {\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  const isReady = verification.verdict === \"READY\";\n  const isFailed = verification.verdict === \"NOT_READY\";\n\n  const verdictText = isReady\n    ? `${theme.fg(\"success\", \"✓ BEREIT\")}`\n    : isFailed\n      ? `${theme.fg(\"error\", \"⚠ NICHT BEREIT\")}`\n      : `${theme.fg(\"muted\", \"○ UNGEPRÜFT\")}`;\n\n  lines.push(crop(`${theme.bold(\"PRÜFUNGEN\")} · ${verdictText}`, width));\n\n  for (const criterion of verification.criteria) {\n    const glyph =\n      criterion.status === \"passed\"\n        ? theme.fg(\"success\", \"✓\")\n        : criterion.status === \"failed\"\n          ? theme.fg(\"error\", \"✗\")\n          : theme.fg(\"muted\", \"○\");\n    lines.push(crop(` ${glyph} ${criterion.label}`, width));\n  }\n\n  const visibleBlockers = (verification.blockers ?? []).slice(0, 3);\n  for (const blocker of visibleBlockers) {\n    lines.push(crop(` ${theme.fg(\"error\", \"Blockiert:\")} ${blocker}`, width));\n  }\n  const hiddenBlockerCount =\n    (verification.blockers?.length ?? 0) - visibleBlockers.length;\n  if (hiddenBlockerCount > 0) {\n    lines.push(\n      crop(theme.fg(\"muted\", ` +${hiddenBlockerCount} weitere`), width),\n    );\n  }\n\n  return lines;\n}\n\n/** Just the title (+ optional goal) line, for callers that already render\n * their own progress bar and current-work detail via the dedicated\n * functions and would otherwise duplicate them through {@link renderTaskWorkspace}. */\nexport function renderTaskHeader(\n  task: TaskViewModel,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n): string[] {\n  const lines = [crop(theme.bold(task.title.toUpperCase()), width)];\n  if (task.goal) {\n    lines.push(crop(theme.fg(\"muted\", task.goal), width));\n  }\n  }\n  return lines;\n}\n\n/** Just the \"Changing: a, b, c\" line, for callers that show the cumulative\n * change list without the rest of {@link renderTaskWorkspace}'s current-work\n * block (e.g. alongside {@link renderActiveTools}, which already covers the\n * currently running tool). */\nexport function renderChangingFiles(\n  work: CurrentWorkViewModel,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n): string[] {\n  if (!work.changingFiles || work.changingFiles.length === 0) return [];\n  return [\n    crop(\n      theme.fg(\"muted\", `Geändert: ${work.changingFiles.join(\", \")}`),\n      width,\n    ),\n  ];\n}\n\nexport function renderTaskWorkspace(\n  task: TaskViewModel,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n): string[] {\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  lines.push(crop(theme.bold(task.title.toUpperCase()), width));\n  lines.push(renderProgressBar(task.phase, theme, width));\n\n  if (task.currentWork) {\n    lines.push(crop(theme.bold(\"AKTUELLE ARBEIT\"), width));\n    lines.push(crop(task.currentWork.summary ?? task.currentWork.title, width));\n    lines.push(...renderChangingFiles(task.currentWork, theme, width));\n  }\n\n  return lines;\n}\n\nexport interface DashboardInput {\n  activityLines: readonly string[];\n  maxRows: number;\n  compact?: boolean;\n}\n\n/** The runtime signals auto mode needs beyond the task view model itself. */\nexport interface AutoDashboardInput {\n  activityLines: readonly string[];\n  layout: Layout;\n  /** At least one tool or subagent is running right now. */\n  hasActiveWork: boolean;\n  /** A completed check no longer describes the workspace (mutation since). */\n  verificationStale: boolean;\n  /** Any verification status is known for this session (a check ran). */\n  verificationKnown: boolean;\n}\n\n/** Row budget for the responsive default dashboard. The regular presentation\n * stays short enough to leave the editor dominant; compact terminals keep the\n * one-line summary plus the most important state. */\nconst AUTO_MAX_ROWS: Record<Layout, number> = {\n  compact: 2,\n  standard: 7,\n  comfortable: 7,\n  wide: 7,\n};\n\n/** The one \"first 3 files, then a +N indicator\" rule, shared by every\n * changed-files preview so the auto and expanded dashboards never disagree\n * on how much detail they show. */\nfunction summarizeChangedFiles(\n  changes: NonNullable<TaskViewModel[\"changesSummary\"]>,\n  separator: string,\n): string {\n  const files = changes.files.slice(0, 3).join(separator);\n  const more = changes.filesCount > 3 ? `, … +${changes.filesCount - 3}` : \"\";\n  return `${files}${more}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * A persistent session overview composed only from the task projection and\n * current runtime activity. The caller owns row budgeting; this renderer keeps\n * tiles intact instead of truncating a frame halfway through.\n *\n * From `wide` width on, tiles are paired side by side (task + activity,\n * changes + verification): a pair costs the height of its taller member, not\n * the sum, so a wide terminal sees a 2×2 card grid. Below that, tiles stack.\n */\nexport function renderDashboard(\n  task: TaskViewModel,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  input: DashboardInput,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const compact = input.compact ?? false;\n  const verification = task.verification;\n  const hasFailure = verification?.verdict === \"NOT_READY\";\n\n  if (compact) {\n    const lines = [\n      crop(\n        `${theme.bold(task.phaseLabel.toUpperCase())} · ${task.title}`,\n        available,\n      ),\n    ];\n    if (hasFailure) {\n      lines.push(\n        crop(theme.fg(\"error\", \"⚠ Prüfung fehlgeschlagen\"), available),\n      );\n    } else if (input.activityLines[0]) {\n      lines.push(crop(input.activityLines[0], available));\n    }\n    return lines.slice(0, Math.max(1, input.maxRows));\n  }\n\n  const showProgress =\n    !hasFailure && (input.activityLines.length === 0 || input.maxRows > 8);\n  const verificationTone =\n    verification?.verdict === \"READY\"\n      ? \"success\"\n      : verification?.verdict === \"NOT_READY\"\n        ? \"error\"\n        : \"muted\";\n  const taskTile: TileInput = {\n    title: \"AUFGABE\",\n    badge: task.phaseLabel.toUpperCase(),\n    tone: \"accent\",\n    fill: NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL,\n    lines: [\n      theme.bold(task.title),\n      ...(task.goal && !hasFailure ? [theme.fg(\"muted\", task.goal)] : []),\n      ...(showProgress\n        ? [renderProgressBar(task.phase, theme, available - 4)]\n        : []),\n    ],\n  };\n  const activityTile: TileInput = {\n    title: \"AKTIVITÄT\",\n    badge: input.activityLines.length > 0 ? \"LÄUFT\" : \"BEREIT\",\n    tone: input.activityLines.length > 0 ? \"accent\" : \"muted\",\n    fill: NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL,\n    lines:\n      input.activityLines.length > 0\n        ? input.activityLines\n        : [\n            theme.fg(\n              \"muted\",\n              task.phase === \"done\"\n                ? \"Letzte Aufgabe abgeschlossen.\"\n                : \"Bereit für die nächste Aufgabe.\",\n            ),\n          ],\n  };\n  const changes = task.changesSummary;\n  const changesTile: TileInput | null = changes\n    ?
