     r.kind === \"investigation\" && r.summary.includes(\"2 Dateien\"),\n              ),\n              \"investigation receipt aggregated\",\n            );\n            assert(\n              receipts.some(\n                (r) =>\n                  r.kind === \"search\" && r.summary.includes(\"1 Suchabfrage\"),\n              ),\n              \"search receipt aggregated\",\n            );\n            assert(\n              receipts.some(\n                (r) =>\n                  r.kind === \"edit\" &&\n                  r.summary === \"1 Datei geändert\" &&\n                  !r.summary.includes(\"+\") &&\n                  r.metrics?.join(\" \") === \"1 files\",\n              ),\n              \"edit receipt reports only the file count supplied by its real event\",\n            );\n\n            // Error preservation\n            agg.recordStart(\"err1\", \"bash\", { command: \"exit 1\" }, 1800);\n            agg.recordEnd(\"err1\", \"Command failed with code 1\", true, 1900);\n            const withError = agg.getReceipts();\n            assert(\n              withError.some(\n                (r) =>\n                  r.status === \"failed\" &&\n                  r.errorDetails?.includes(\"failed with code 1\"),\n              ),\n              \"tool errors are preserved in receipts\",\n            );\n\n            // 1b. detailRef resolves back to the underlying raw record via\n            // getRecord() — investigation/search receipts are built from real\n            // recordStart/recordEnd calls above, so their detailRef must\n            // resolve, and an error receipt's detailRef must point at the\n            // record that actually failed.\n            const investigationReceipt = withError.find(\n              (r) => r.kind === \"investigation\",\n            );\n            assert(\n              investigationReceipt?.detailRef &&\n                agg.getRecord(investigationReceipt.detailRef)?.toolName ===\n                  \"read\",\n              \"investigation receipt's detailRef resolves back to a real read record\",\n            );\n            const searchReceipt = withError.find((r) => r.kind === \"search\");\n            assert(\n              searchReceipt?.detailRef &&\n                agg.getRecord(searchReceipt.detailRef)?.toolName === \"grep\",\n              \"search receipt's detailRef resolves back to a real grep record\",\n            );\n            const errorReceipt = withError.find(\n              (r) => r.status === \"failed\" && r.kind === \"generic\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              errorReceipt?.detailRef,\n              \"err1\",\n              \"error receipt's detailRef is the id of the record that failed\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              agg.getRecord(\"err1\")?.toolName,\n              \"bash\",\n              \"the failing record itself resolves via getRecord\",\n            );\n\n            // 1c. An edit receipt built from a real tool record carries a\n            // resolvable detailRef pointing at the last-touched file's record.\n            const editAgg = new ReceiptAggregator();\n            editAgg.recordStart(\"e1\", \"edit\", { path: \"src/app.ts\" }, 100);\n            editAgg.recordEnd(\"e1\", \"ok\", false, 200);\n            const editReceipt = editAgg\n              .getReceipts()\n              .find((r) => r.kind === \"edit\");\n            assert(\n              editReceipt?.detailRef === \"e1\" &&\n                editAgg.getRecord(\"e1\")?.args?.path === \"src/app.ts\",\n              \"edit receipt's detailRef resolves to the record for the last touched file\",\n            );\n\n            // 1d. The recent-records ring buffer is bounded: the oldest record\n            // is evicted once it fills up, but that never removes the receipt\n            // itself or the error detail already embedded inline on it.\n            const ringAgg = new ReceiptAggregator();\n            ringAgg.recordStart(\"first\", \"bash\", { command: \"echo 1\" }, 0);\n            ringAgg.recordEnd(\"first\", \"1\", false, 1);\n            for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) {\n              ringAgg.recordStart(\n                `fill-${i}`,\n                \"bash\",\n                { command: \"noop\" },\n                i + 2,\n              );\n              ringAgg.recordEnd(`fill-${i}`, \"ok\", false, i + 3);\n            }\n            eq(\n              ringAgg.getRecord(\"first\"),\n              undefined,\n              \"the oldest record is evicted once the recent-records ring buffer fills up\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              ringAgg.getRecord(\"fill-24\")?.toolName,\n              \"bash\",\n              \"the most recently completed record is still resolvable\",\n            );\n\n            const ringErrAgg = new ReceiptAggregator();\n            ringErrAgg.recordStart(\n              \"early-error\",\n              \"bash\",\n              { command: \"exit 1\" },\n              0,\n            );\n            ringErrAgg.recordEnd(\"early-error\", \"boom\", true, 1);\n            for (let i = 0; i < 25; i++) {\n              ringErrAgg.recordStart(\n                `noise-${i}`,\n                \"bash\",\n                { command: \"ok\" },\n                i + 2,\n              );\n              ringErrAgg.recordEnd(`noise-${i}`, \"ok\", false, i + 3);\n            }\n            const survivingErrorReceipt = ringErrAgg\n              .getReceipts()\n              .find((r) => r.id === \"early-error\");\n            assert(\n              survivingErrorReceipt?.errorDetails?.includes(\"boom\"),\n              \"an error receipt's errorDetails survives ring-buffer eviction of its raw record\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              ringErrAgg.getRecord(\"early-error\"),\n              undefined,\n              \"only the stale detailRef lookup is lost on eviction, never the receipt itself\",\n            );\n\n            // 2. Task Phase determination\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"simple_plan\",\n                activityKind: \"tool\",\n                verificationStatus: null,\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"plan\",\n              \"plan mode maps to plan phase\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"thinking\",\n                verificationStatus: null,\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"understand\",\n              \"work thinking maps to understand phase\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"tool\",\n                verificationStatus: null,\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"work\",\n              \"work tool maps to work phase\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"idle\",\n                verificationStatus: \"checks_failed\",\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"verify\",\n              \"failed verification maps to verify phase\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"idle\",\n                verificationStatus: \"verified\",\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"done\",\n              \"verified status maps to done phase\",\n            );\n\n            // 2b. Phase/verification contradiction matrix\n            // (pi-tui-optimization-package/04-test-matrix.md, automated state tests)\n            const noTools = new Map();\n            const editTool = new Map([\n              [\n                \"t1\",\n                {\n                  id: \"t1\",\n                  name: \"edit\",\n                  kind: \"edit\",\n                  target: \"src/main.ts\",\n                  startedAt: Date.now(),\n                },\n              ],\n            ]);\n            const verifyTool = new Map([\n              [\n                \"v1\",\n                {\n                  id: \"v1\",\n                  name: \"project_check\",\n                  kind: \"verification\",\n                  startedAt: Date.now(),\n                },\n              ],\n            ]);\n\n            // Scenario 1: idle after current successful verification.\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"idle\",\n                verificationStatus: \"verified\",\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n                verificationIsCurrent: true,\n              }).phase,\n              \"done\",\n            );\n            eq(projectVerificationState(\"verified\", noTools).verdict, \"READY\");\n\n            // Scenario 2: edit starts after successful verification — active work,\n            // never a running check, and the verdict turns unverified.\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"tool\",\n                verificationStatus: \"verified\",\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n                verificationIsCurrent: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"work\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              projectVerificationState(\"verified\", editTool).verdict,\n              \"UNVERIFIED\",\n            );\n\n            // Scenario 3: edit finished after successful verification (idle again,\n            // but the check is now stale) — must not be done.\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"idle\",\n                verificationStatus: \"verified\",\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n                verificationIsCurrent: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"work\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              projectVerificationState(\"verified\", noTools, null, true).verdict,\n              \"UNVERIFIED\",\n            );\n\n            // Scenario 4: required check fails while idle — an invitation to\n            // re-check, with the failure verdict, never a fabricated success.\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"idle\",\n                verificationStatus: \"checks_failed\",\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n                verificationIsCurrent: true,\n              }).phase,\n              \"verify\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              projectVerificationState(\"checks_failed\", noTools).verdict,\n              \"NOT_READY\",\n            );\n\n            // Scenario 5: editing to fix the failed check stays work — the earlier\n            // failure must not present as a currently running verification. The\n            // existing verdict contract keeps a structured failed summary at\n            // NOT_READY and degrades an unstructured stale one to UNVERIFIED.\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"tool\",\n                verificationStatus: \"checks_failed\",\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n                verificationIsCurrent: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"work\",\n            );\n            assert(\n              [\"NOT_READY\", \"UNVERIFIED\"].includes(\n                projectVerificationState(\"checks_failed\", editTool).verdict,\n              ),\n              \"an edit after a failed check never fabricates READY\",\n            );\n\n            // Scenario 6: only a real running verification tool claims verify,\n            // and its in-flight result is unverified.\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"work\",\n                activityKind: \"tool\",\n                verificationStatus: null,\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: true,\n                verificationIsCurrent: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"verify\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              projectVerificationState(null, verifyTool).verdict,\n              \"UNVERIFIED\",\n            );\n\n            // Scenarios 7 & 8: planning keeps its semantics without fabricating\n            // a verification result.\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"simple_plan\",\n                activityKind: \"thinking\",\n                verificationStatus: null,\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"understand\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              determineTaskPhase({\n                mode: \"detailed_plan\",\n                activityKind: \"tool\",\n                verificationStatus: null,\n                hasActiveVerificationTool: false,\n              }).phase,\n              \"plan\",\n            );\n            eq(projectVerificationState(null, noTools, null, false), undefined);\n\n            // Invariant across every activity × status × staleness combination:\n            // a done phase requires verified + current (no mutation since).\n            for (const kind of [\"thinking\", \"tool\", \"responding\", \"idle\"]) {\n              for (const status of [\n                null,\n                \"verified\",\n                \"checks_failed\",\n                \"unchanged\",\n              ]) {\n                for (const current of [true, false]) {\n                  for (const hasVerify of [true, false]) {\n                    const { phase } = determineTaskPhase({\n                      mode: \"work\",\n                      activityKind: kind,\n                      verificationStatus: status,\n                      hasActiveVerificationTool: hasVerify,\n                      verificationIsCurrent: current,\n                    });\n                    if (phase !== \"done\") continue;\n                    assert(\n                      status === \"verified\" && current && !hasVerify,\n                      `phase done implies READY and current verification (kind=${kind} status=${status} current=${current})`,\n                    );\n                  }\n                }\n              }\n            }\n            const doneTvm = projectTaskViewModel({\n              state: {\n                sessionEpoch: \"test-epoch\",\n                workflow: { phase: \"work\", label: \"Work\" },\n                permissions: {},\n                lsp: {},\n                model: { id: \"test-model\", thinking: \"medium\" },\n                activity: { kind: \"idle\" },\n              },\n              activeTools: noTools,\n              subagents: [],\n              verificationStatus: \"verified\",\n              workspaceChangedSinceVerification: false,\n              now: Date.now(),\n            });\n            assert(\n              doneTvm.phase === \"done\" &&\n                doneTvm.verification?.verdict === \"READY\",\n              \"the projected view model pairs done with a READY verdict only\",\n            );\n\n            // 3. Task Title extraction\n            eq(\n              extractTaskTitle(\n                undefined,\n                \"# Fix Compaction\\n\\nZiel: threshold fix\",\n              ).title,\n              \"Fix Compaction\",\n              \"plan title extracted\",\n            );\n            eq(\n              extractTaskTitle(\"/work Fix the broken test\").title,\n              \"Fix the broken test\",\n              \"prompt title extracted\",\n            );\n\n            // 4. Progress bar rendering\n            const pb = renderProgressBar(\"work\", context.ui.theme, 120);\n            assert(\n              pb.includes(\"Verstehen\") &&\n                pb.includes(\"Arbeiten\") &&\n                pb.includes(\"Fertig\"),\n              \"progress bar renders all phases\",\n            );\n\n            // 5. Subagent branch rendering\n            const subagentBranches = renderSubagentBranches(\n              [\n                {\n                  agent: \"investigator\",\n                  status: \"completed\",\n                  focus: \"Found 2 root causes\",\n                },\n                {\n                  agent: \"verifier\",\n                  status: \"running\",\n                  focus: \"Running tests\",\n                },\n              ],\n              context.ui.theme,\n              120,\n            );\n            assert(\n              subagentBranches.some((l) => l.includes(\"investigator\")) &&\n                subagentBranches.some((l) => l.includes(\"Found 2 root causes\")),\n              \"subagent branch rendered with findings\",\n            );\n\n            // 5b. Every SubagentBranchInfo status renders a distinct, labeled\n            // line — including \"paused\", which previously had no branch\n            // rendering at all and fell through projectSubagentBranches as a\n            // mislabeled \"completed\" (see the regression test below).\n            for (const status of [\n              \"running\",\n              \"paused\",\n              \"completed\",\n              \"failed\",\n              \"needs_attention\",\n              \"queued\",\n            ]) {\n              const lines = renderSubagentBranches(\n                [{ agent: \"a\", status }],\n                context.ui.theme,\n                120,\n              );\n              assert(\n                lines.length > 0 && lines[0].includes(\"a\"),\n                `renderSubagentBranches renders a \"${status}\" branch`,\n              );\n            }\n\n            // 5c. projectSubagentBranches maps every live SubagentInfo status\n            // explicitly — \"paused\" must not fall through to \"completed\".\n            const pausedBranch = projectSubagentBranches([\n              { agent: \"investigator\", status: \"paused\" },\n            ])[0];\n            eq(\n              pausedBranch.status,\n              \"paused\",\n              \"a paused subagent is projected as paused, not completed\",\n            );\n            const otherStatuses = projectSubagentBranches([\n              { agent: \"a\", status: \"running\" },\n              { agent: \"b\", status: \"needs_attention\" },\n              { agent: \"c\", status: \"queued\" },\n            ]).map((b) => b.status);\n            eq(\n              otherStatuses.join(\",\"),\n              \"running,needs_attention,queued\",\n              \"the other live subagent statuses still map straight through\",\n            );\n\n            // 6. Verification block rendering\n            const verifyBlock = renderVerificationBlock(\n              {\n                verdict: \"READY\",\n                criteria: [{ label: \"Unit tests passing\", status: \"passed\" }],\n                blockers: [],\n              },\n              context.ui.theme,\n              120,\n            );\n            assert(\n              verifyBlock.some(\n                (l) => l.includes(\"PRÜFUNGEN\") && l.includes(\"BEREIT\"),\n              ),\n              \"verification block renders ready verdict\",\n            );\n\n            // 7. Inspector box rendering\n            const inspectorBox = renderInspectorBox(\n              {\n                title: \"CHANGES\",\n                badge: \"3 files\",\n                sections: [\n                  { title: \"Modified\", lines: [\"src/main.ts (+10 -2)\"] },\n                ],\n                actions: [{ label: \"Open Diff\", key: \"Enter\" }],\n              },\n              context.ui.theme,\n              80,\n            );\n            assert(\n              inspectorBox.some(\n                (l) => l.includes(\"CHANGES\") && l.includes(\"3 files\"),\n              ),\n              \"inspector box renders header and badge\",\n            );\n\n            // 8. Full task view model & workspace rendering\n            const tvm = projectTaskViewModel({\n              state: {\n                sessionEpoch: \"test-epoch\",\n                workflow: { phase: \"work\", label: \"Work\" },\n                permissions: {},\n                lsp: {},\n                model: { id: \"test-model\", thinking: \"medium\" },\n                activity: { kind: \"tool\" },\n              },\n              activeTools: new Map([\n                [\n                  \"t1\",\n                  {\n                    id: \"t1\",\n                    name: \"edit\",\n                    kind: \"edit\",\n                    target: \"src/main.ts\",\n                    startedAt: Date.now() - 5000,\n                  },\n                ],\n              ]),\n              subagents: [],\n              receiptAggregator: agg,\n              now: Date.now(),\n            });\n            eq(tvm.phase, \"work\", \"task view model computes correct phase\");\n            const workspaceLines = renderTaskWorkspace(\n              tvm,\n              context.ui.theme,\n              120,\n            );\n            assert(\n              workspaceLines.some((l) => l.includes(\"AKTUELLE ARBEIT\")),\n              \"renderTaskWorkspace includes current work block\",\n            );\n\n            const idleDashboard = renderDashboard(\n              {\n                ...tvm,\n                phase: \"done\",\n                phaseLabel: \"Fertig\",\n                currentWork: undefined,\n              },\n              context.ui.theme,\n              120,\n              { activityLines: [], maxRows: 8 },\n            );\n            assert(\n              idleDashboard.some((line) => line.includes(\"AUFGABE\")) &&\n                idleDashboard.some((line) =>\n                  line.includes(\"Letzte Aufgabe abgeschlossen.\"),\n                ),\n              \"the dashboard remains useful after a turn has settled\",\n            );\n            // Wide terminals pair tiles side by side: task + verification cost\n            // the taller member's height (4 rows), the activity/changes pair\n            // 3–4 more. The failure verdict stays visible at every budget;\n            // routine tiles join only once the grid actually fits the budget.\n            for (const maxRows of [5, 6, 7, 8]) {\n              const activityFits = maxRows >= 7;\n              const failedDashboard = renderDashboard(\n                {\n                  ...tvm,\n                  verification: {\n                    verdict: \"NOT_READY\",\n                    criteria: [{ label: \"Tests\", status: \"failed\" }],\n                    blockers: [\"Pflichtprüfung fehlgeschlagen.\"],\n                  },\n                },\n                context.ui.theme,\n                120,\n                { activityLines: [\"ARBEITET\"], maxRows },\n              );\n              assert(\n                failedDashboard.some((line) => line.includes(\"NICHT BEREIT\")) &&\n                  failedDashboard.some((line) => line.includes(\"AKTIVITÄT\")) ===\n                    activityFits,\n                `a failed verification survives the ${maxRows}-row budget${\n                  activityFits\n                    ? \" while the grid still fits routine activity\"\n                    : \" before routine activity\"\n                }`,\n              );\n              const changesFits = maxRows >= 8;\n              const failedDashboardWithChanges = renderDashboard(\n                {\n                  ...tvm,\n                  changesSummary: {\n                    filesCount: 1,\n                    files: [\"src/a.ts\"],\n                    linesAdded: 1,\n                    linesRemoved: 0,\n                  },\n                  verification: {\n                    verdict: \"NOT_READY\",\n                    criteria: [{ label: \"Tests\", status: \"failed\" }],\n                    blockers: [\"Pflichtprüfung fehlgeschlagen.\"],\n                  },\n                },\n                context.ui.theme,\n                120,\n                { activityLines: [], maxRows },\n              );\n              assert(\n                failedDashboardWithChanges.some((line) =>\n                  line.includes(\"NICHT BEREIT\"),\n                ) &&\n                  failedDashboardWithChanges.some((line) =>\n                    line.includes(\"ÄNDERUNGEN\"),\n                  ) ===\n                    changesFits,\n                `a failed verification survives the ${maxRows}-row budget${\n                  changesFits\n                    ? \" while the grid still fits the changes tile\"\n                    : \" before changes\"\n                }`,\n              );\n            }\n            const compactDashboard = renderDashboard(\n              tvm,\n              context.ui.theme,\n              45,\n              {\n                activityLines: [\"ARBEITET · 1s\"],\n                maxRows: 2,\n                compact: true,\n              },\n            );\n            assert(\n              compactDashboard.length <= 2 &&\n                compactDashboard.some((line) => line.includes(\"ARBEITEN\")),\n              \"the compact dashboard preserves the current phase in two rows\",\n            );\n\n            // 8c. Responsive permanent auto-dashboard matrix.\n            const failedAuto = renderAutoDashboard(\n              {\n                ...tvm,\n                verification: {\n                  verdict: \"NOT_READY\",\n                  criteria: [{ label: \"Tests\", status: \"failed\" }],\n                  blockers: ['Pflichtprüfung \"test\" ist fehlgeschlagen.'],\n                },\n              },\n              context.ui.theme,\n              100,\n              {\n                activityLines: [\"ARBEITET · 9s\"],\n                layout: \"standard\",\n                hasActiveWork:         failedAuto.some((line) => line.includes(\"ist fehlgeschlagen\")) &&\n                !failedAutoText.includes(\"Prüfung · Nicht bereit\"),\n              \"auto mode names the most relevant context.ui.theme,\n              100,\n              {\n                activityLines: [],\n                layout: \"standard\",\n                hasActiveWork: false,\n             !staleAuto.some((line) => stripAnsi(line).includes(\"Fertig\")),\n              \"a stale check is reported as open once, never as finished\",\n            );\n\n            const          },\n              context.ui.theme,\n              100,\n              {\n                activityLines: [],\n                layout: \"standard\",\n                hasActiveWork: false,\n changesAuto.some((line) =>\n                  stripAnsi(line).includes(\"4 Dateien\"),\n                ) &&\n                changesAuto.some((line) => stripAnsi(line).includes(\"+30 −5\")) &&\n   );\n\n            const cleanIdleAuto = renderAutoDashboard(\n              { ...tvm, phase: \"work\", phaseLabel: \"Bereit\" },\n              context.ui.theme,\n              100,\n              {\n                activityLines: [],\n                layout: \"standard\",\n                hasActiveWork: false,\n                verificationStale: false,\n                verificationKnown:     const narrowAuto = renderAutoDashboard(tvm, context.ui.theme, 40, {\n              activityLines: [\"ARBEITET · 4s\", \"◌ Lesen README.md\"],\n              layout: \"compact\",\n {"type":"item.completed","item":{"id":"item_20","type":"command_execution","command":"/usr/bin/zsh -lc \"rg --files -g 'package-lock.json' -g 'npm-shrinkwrap.json' -g 'pnpm-lock.yaml' -g 'yarn.lock' && sed -n '1,180p' extensions/aurora-ui/tile.ts && sed -n '180,300p' extensions/aurora-ui/tile.ts && sed -n '1030,1170p' extensions/aurora-ui/tool-renderers.ts && sed -n '1,150p' themes/aurora-night.json\"","aggregated_output":"gui/package-lock.json\nnpm/packages/frontend-protocol/package-lock.json\nnpm/package-lock.json\nimport type { Theme } from \"@earendil-works/pi-coding-agent\";\nimport { visibleWidth } from \"@earendil-works/pi-tui\";\nimport type { Tone } from \"../shared/ui-theme.ts\";\nimport { crop } from \"./layout.ts\";\n\n/**\n * The only background fills Aurora ever paints. Pi's public `Theme.bg` accepts\n * exactly these eight tokens, so tiles and pills stay correct in every theme\n * (including `light`) instead of hardcoding ANSI colours. The type is derived\n * from the API itself: `ThemeBg` is not exported by the package entry.\n */\nexport type TileFill = Parameters<Theme[\"bg\"]>[0];\n\nexport interface TileInput {\n  title: string;\n  lines: readonly string[];\n  badge?: string;\n  tone?: \"accent\" | \"muted\" | \"warning\" | \"error\" | \"success\";\n  /** Card background. Omit it and the tile degrades to the flat panel look. */\n  fill?: TileFill;\n}\n\n/** The neutral card surface every tile uses unless a status owns its fill. */\nexport const NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL: TileFill = \"toolPendingBg\";\n\n/**\n * Maps a status tone to the card fill that carries it. Only success and error\n * have dedicated background tokens; everything else sits on the neutral card.\n */\nexport function statusFill(tone: TileInput[\"tone\"]): TileFill {\n  if (tone === \"success\") return \"toolSuccessBg\";\n  if (tone === \"error\") return \"toolErrorBg\";\n  return NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL;\n}\n\n/**\n * A status chip. Loud tones get a filled background (or inverse, since Pi has\n * no warning background token); routine tones stay flat text so a bar of many\n * segments does not turn into a wall of colour.\n */\nexport function renderPill(theme: Theme, text: string, tone: Tone): string {\n  switch (tone) {\n    case \"accent\":\n      return theme.bg(\n        \"selectedBg\",\n        ` ${theme.fg(\"accent\", theme.bold(text))} `,\n      );\n    case \"success\":\n      return theme.bg(\"toolSuccessBg\", ` ${theme.fg(\"success\", text)} `);\n    case \"error\":\n      return theme.bg(\n        \"toolErrorBg\",\n        ` ${theme.fg(\"error\", theme.bold(text))} `,\n      );\n    case \"warning\":\n      return theme.inverse(` ${theme.fg(\"warning\", text)} `);\n    default:\n      return theme.fg(tone, text);\n  }\n}\n\nexport interface ShortcutItem {\n  key: string;\n  label: string;\n}\n\n/**\n * Renders key hints as a responsive sequence of complete units.  A shortcut\n * never enters a row until both its key cap and its (possibly shortened)\n * description fit; this prevents the final background-painted cell of a cap\n * from being clipped at a card edge.\n */\nexport function renderShortcutRows(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  shortcuts: readonly ShortcutItem[],\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const units = shortcuts.map(({ key, label }) => {\n    const cap = renderPill(theme, key, \"accent\");\n    const capWidth = visibleWidth(cap);\n    // A framed Aurora card is only used from 18 columns on, so every key cap\n    // has room here. Keep the cap atomic even if a caller uses this primitive\n    // in a smaller, frameless surface.\n    if (capWidth >= available) return crop(cap, available);\n    const labelWidth = Math.max(0, available - capWidth - 1);\n    const description = labelWidth > 0\n      ? crop(theme.fg(\"muted\", label), labelWidth)\n      : \"\";\n    return description ? `${cap} ${description}` : cap;\n  });\n\n  const rows: string[] = [];\n  let row = \"\";\n  for (const unit of units) {\n    const candidate = row ? `${row}  ${unit}` : unit;\n    if (row && visibleWidth(candidate) > available) {\n      rows.push(row);\n      row = unit;\n    } else {\n      row = candidate;\n    }\n  }\n  if (row) rows.push(row);\n  return rows;\n}\n\n/** Terminal cells a pill occupies: its text plus one padding cell each side. */\nexport function pillExtraCells(tone: Tone): number {\n  return tone === \"accent\" ||\n    tone === \"success\" ||\n    tone === \"error\" ||\n    tone === \"warning\"\n    ? 2\n    : 0;\n}\n\n/**\n * A labelled field row: muted uppercase label, then the value. Callers style\n * the value themselves; the tile crops the finished row to its inner width.\n */\nexport function renderField(\n  theme: Theme,\n  label: string,\n  value: string,\n): string {\n  return `${theme.fg(\"muted\", theme.bold(label.toUpperCase()))}  ${value}`;\n}\n\nfunction padFilled(\n  theme: Theme,\n  value: string,\n  width: number,\n  fill: TileFill,\n): string {\n  // crop closes any fg/bold left open by the truncation, so the tile fill is\n  // not punctured by a stray full reset and no colour bleeds past this row\n  // into the frame or a neighbouring tile.\n  const clipped = crop(value, width);\n  const padding = \" \".repeat(Math.max(0, width - visibleWidth(clipped)));\n  return `${theme.bg(fill, clipped)}${theme.bg(fill, padding)}`;\n}\n\n/**\n * A title row reserves its badge first.  The earlier `${title}${badge}` crop\n * made a long title consume the entire row and leave the state badge absent,\n * even though the badge is the more useful scan target.\n */\nfunction renderTileHeading(\n  theme: Theme,\n  title: string,\n  badge: string | undefined,\n  tone: NonNullable<TileInput[\"tone\"]>,\n  width: number,\n): string {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const renderedBadge = badge\n    ? ` ${renderPill(theme, badge, tone)}`\n    : \"\";\n  const titleWidth = Math.max(1, available - visibleWidth(renderedBadge));\n  const renderedTitle = crop(theme.fg(tone, theme.bold(title)), titleWidth);\n  return `${renderedTitle}${renderedBadge}`;\n}\n\n/** One empty, filled card row — used to equalise tile heights in a grid. */\nexport function tileBlankRow(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  fill?: TileFill,\n): string {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  if (!fill || available < 18) return \"\";\n  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, available - 4);\n  const border = (value: string) => theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", value);\n  return `${border(\"│ \")}${padFilled(theme, \"\", innerWidth, fill)}${border(\" │\")}`;\n}\n\n/** Card rows: two frame rows plus one row per content line. */\nexport function tileHeight(input: TileInput): number {\n  return input.lines.length + 2;\n  return input.lines.length + 2;\n}\n\n/**\n * A filled card: the frame shell, but the title row and every content row are\n * padded to the full inner width and painted with `theme.bg`, so the card\n * reads as one solid surface. The < 18 column fallback drops the frame\n * entirely and preserves the content, as the old panel shell did.\n */\nexport function renderTile(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  input: TileInput,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const tone = input.tone ?? \"accent\";\n  const heading = renderTileHeading(\n    theme,\n    input.title,\n    input.badge,\n    tone,\n    Math.max(1, available - 4),\n  );\n\n  if (available < 18) {\n    return [\n      crop(heading, available),\n      ...input.lines.map((line) => crop(line, available)),\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const innerWidth = Math.max(1, available - 4);\n  const border = (value: string) => theme.fg(\"borderMuted\", value);\n\n  if (!input.fill) {\n    // Unfilled tiles keep the classic framed look without a background.\n    const clippedHeading = crop(heading, innerWidth);\n    const fillDashes = \"─\".repeat(\n      Math.max(0, innerWidth - visibleWidth(clippedHeading)),\n    );\n    const row = (value: string) => {\n      const clipped = crop(value, innerWidth);\n      return `${border(\"│ \")}${clipped}${\" \".repeat(\n        Math.max(0, innerWidth - visibleWidth(clipped)),\n      )}${border(\" │\")}`;\n    };\n    return [\n      `${border(\"╭─\")}${clippedHeading}${border(`${fillDashes}─╮`)}`,\n      ...input.lines.map(row),\n      border(`╰${\"─\".repeat(Math.max(1, available - 2))}╯`),\n    ];\n  }\n\n  const fill = input.fill;\n  const filledHeading = padFilled(theme, heading, innerWidth, fill);\n  const row = (value: string) =>\n    `${border(\"│ \")}${padFilled(theme, value, innerWidth, fill)}${border(\" │\")}`;\n\n  return [\n    `${border(\"╭─\")}${filledHeading}${border(\"─╮\")}`,\n    ...input.lines.map(row),\n    border(`╰${\"─\".repeat(Math.max(1, available - 2))}╯`),\n  ];\n}\n\n/**\n * Lays tiles out as a grid. With `columns: 2` consecutive tiles share a row:\n * both get half the width, the shorter one is padded with blank filled rows.\n * An unpaired last tile spans the full width. Widths under two framed tiles\n * degrade to a vertical stack, and a single column always stacks.\n */\nexport function renderTileGrid(\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  tiles: readonly TileInput[],\n  columns: 1 | 2 = 1,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  // Two framed tiles side by side need 2 × 18 columns plus one gap column.\n  const framedPairFits = available >= 37;\n  if (columns !== 2 || !framedPairFits) {\n    return tiles.flatMap((tile) => renderTile(theme, available, tile));\n  }\n\n  const lines: string[] = [];\n  // Split the content width exactly. Using the same floored width for both\n  // sides used to leave the final cell blank whenever `available` was even,\n  // making a pair of filled cards look like their frames and backgrounds had\n  // different right boundaries.\n  const leftWidth = Math.floor((available - 1) / 2);\n  const rightWidth = available - leftWidth - 1;\n  for (let index = 0; index < tiles.length; index += 2) {\n    const left = tiles[index]!;\n    const right = tiles[index + 1];\n    if (!right) {\n      lines.push(...renderTile(theme, available, left));\n      continue;\n    }\n    const leftLines = renderTile(theme, leftWidth, left);\n    const rightLines = renderTile(theme, rightWidth, right);\n    const height = Math.max(leftLines.length, rightLines.length);\n    for (let row = 0; row < height; row += 1) {\n      const leftLine =\n        leftLines[row] ?? tileBlankRow(theme, leftWidth, left.fill);\n      const rightLine =\n        rightLines[row] ?? tileBlankRow(theme, rightWidth, right.fill);\n      lines.push(`${leftLine} ${rightLine}`);\n    }\n  }\n  return lines;\n}\n      ? Math.max(...group.map((tile) => tileHeight(tile)))\n      : group.reduce((sum, tile) => sum + tileHeight(tile), 0);\n\n  const chosen: TileInput[][] = [];\n  let usedRows = 0;\n  for (const group of groups) {\n    const height = groupHeight(group);\n    if (chosen.length > 0 && usedRows + height > input.maxRows) continue;\n    chosen.push(group);\n    usedRows += height;\n  }\n  if (chosen.length === 0) return renderTile(theme, available, taskTile);\n  return chosen.flatMap((group) =>\n    renderTileGrid(theme, available, group, grid ? 2 : 1),\n  );\n}\n\n/**\n * Aurora's responsive default presentation. Unlike the old state-dependent\n * auto mode, it remains useful after a turn settles: one small session card\n * gives task, activity, changes and verification stable places. Only the\n * compact width tier falls back to two unframed rows.\n */\nexport function renderAutoDashboard(\n  task: TaskViewModel,\n  theme: Theme,\n  width: number,\n  input: AutoDashboardInput,\n): string[] {\n  const available = Math.max(1, width);\n  const budget = AUTO_MAX_ROWS[input.layout];\n  const clip = (value: string) => crop(value, available);\n  const verification = task.verification;\n  const failed = verification?.verdict === \"NOT_READY\";\n  const stale =\n    !failed &&\n    task.phase !== \"verify\" &&\n    input.verificationStale &&\n    (verification?.verdict !== undefined || input.verificationKnown);\n  const taskLine = theme.bold(task.title);\n\n  const problemLines: string[] = [];\n  if (failed) {\n    problemLines.push(theme.fg(\"error\", theme.bold(\"⚠ Prüfung fehlgeschlagen\")));\n    const blocker = verification?.blockers?.[0];\n    if (blocker) problemLines.push(theme.fg(\"muted\", `  ${blocker}`));\n  } else if (stale) {\n    problemLines.push(\n      theme.fg(\"warning\", \"○ Prüfung offen · Änderungen seit dem letzten Lauf\"),\n    );\n  }\n\n  // Live rows exist only during real work; idle sessions get no readiness\n  // claim of their own — the panel badge already names the phase.\n  // The caller already provides one canonical activity heading (including its\n  // state and elapsed time). Prefixing every line with a second “Aktivität”\n  // marker made active cards read like two competing status systems.\n  const activityLines = input.hasActiveWork ? input.activityLines : [];\n  const changes = task.changesSummary;\n  // Routine rows only ever appear when they carry state: \"no changes yet\" and\n  // \"never checked\" are zero statements and stay off the dashboard.\n  const changesLine = changes\n    ? `${theme.fg(\"accent\", \"◇ Änderungen\")} · ${changes.filesCount} ${changes.filesCount === 1 ? \"Datei\" : \"Dateien\"} · ${theme.fg(\"success\", `+${changes.linesAdded}`)} ${theme.fg(\"error\", `−${changes.linesRemoved}`)}`\n    : undefined;\n  // Only a successful, current verification is routine information. Failed\n  // and stale states already have a higher-priority problem line; UNVERIFIED\n  // is a zero statement and must not consume dashboard space.\n  const verdictLine =\n    verification?.verdict === \"READY\"\n      ? theme.fg(\"success\", \"✓ Prüfung · Bereit\")\n      : undefined;\n  const idleSegments = [\n    changes && changes.filesCount > 0\n      ? `${theme.fg(\"accent\", \"◇\")} ${changes.filesCount} ${changes.filesCount === 1 ? \"Datei\" : \"Dateien\"} · ${theme.fg(\"success\", `+${changes.linesAdded}`)} ${theme.fg(\"error\", `−${changes.linesRemoved}`)}`\n      : undefined,\n    verdictLine,\n  ].filter((segment) => segment !== undefined);\n  const idleSummary =\n    idleSegments.length > 0 ? idleSegments.join(\" · \") : undefined;\n\n  if (input.layout === \"compact\") {\n    // Two unframed rows are the whole budget here: the phase line plus at most\n    // one information-bearing row. An unrun check stays silent rather than\n    // spending half the dashboard on a zero statement.\n    const secondRow = problemLines[0] ?? activityLines[0] ?? idleSummary;\n    return [\n      clip(`${task.phaseLabel.toUpperCase()} · ${theme.bold(task.title)}`),\n      ...(secondRow !== undefined ? [clip(secondRow)] : []),\n    ].slice(0, budget);\n  }\n\n  // A frame costs two rows. Select full sections before framing so a terminal\n  // never receives a cropped box and failures retain precedence over routine\n  // activity and metadata.\n  const contentBudget = Math.max(1, budget - 2);\n  // A failure or stale line already owns the verification meaning. Do not\n  // repeat it as a second generic verification row; spend that row on the\n  // active work or change summary instead. Idle collapses routine metadata\n  // into one condensed line — or none, when nothing has a state to report.\n  const routineLines = (\n    problemLines.length > 0\n      ? [changesLine]\n      : input.hasActiveWork\n        ? [changesLine, verdictLine]\n        : [idleSummary]\n  ).filter((line) => line !== undefined);\n  const activityBudget = Math.max(\n    1,\n    contentBudget - 1 - problemLines.length - routineLines.length,\n  );\n  const content = [\n    taskLine,\n    ...problemLines,\n    ...activityLines.slice(0, activityBudget),\n    ...routineLines,\n  ]\n    .slice(0, contentBudget)\n    .map(clip);\n  return renderTile(theme, available, {\n    title: \"Sitzung\",\n    badge: task.phaseLabel.toUpperCase(),\n    tone: failed ? \"error\" : stale ? \"warning\" : \"accent\",\n    fill: failed ? \"toolErrorBg\" : NEUTRAL_TILE_FILL,\n    lines: content,\n  });\n}\n{\n  \"$schema\":
